Despite rapid adoption by major vendors and economically meaningful mainnet activity, the security posture of real-world x402 deployments remains poorly characterized.
  We present the first systematic study of authorization correctness and execution safety in current facilitator-mediated x402 deployments in the wild, identifying eight security rules for facilitators as critical payment infrastructure. Based on our analysis of rule violations, we derive four new attack vectors, including Free Shopping, Asset Theft, Service Denial, and Gas Abuse. These attacks exploit weaknesses in the real-world facilitator and server implementations and cause severe harm, including direct financial loss to merchants, theft of facilitator-held assets, unbounded sponsor-paid gas/fees, and disruption of payment services. To assess the security of x402 deployments at scale, we propose a semi-automated black-box tool and apply it to 15 major x402 facilitators collectively used by over 60K sellers and 360K buyers. Alarmingly, we find violations in all evaluated facilitators. We responsibly disclosed our findings to the affected parties, who acknowledged the issues and adopted mitigations, including changes by Coinbase. Finally, we complement our controlled testing with an empirical measurement of over 119 million recent Base and Solana transactions, quantifying x402 adoption, facilitator centralization, and ecosystem-level risk indicators.

</details>


### [131] [Examining User Behavior and Cognitive Biases in Personal Password Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19586)
*Evelyn Crowe,Patralika Ghosh,Shreyas Kumar,Rebecca Schlegel,Rebecca Ward,Guofei Gu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 用户尽管意识到网络安全风险，但由于心理偏见（如双曲贴现、现状偏见、现时偏见），仍优先考虑即时便利而非长期安全，导致不良密码行为持续存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究用户持续存在不安全密码行为（如密码重用、弱凭证、忽视安全建议）的行为和认知原因，理解安全意识到安全行动之间的差距。

Method: 通过问卷调查，分析用户创建、存储和管理密码的方式，并检验随时间推移安全习惯是否因意识增强而改善；引入行为经济学视角（双曲贴现、现状偏见、现时偏见）解释决策过程。

Result: 即时便利性往往优先于长期安全考虑，用户倾向于选择可记忆性而非密码强度；关键心理偏见导致安全拖延和对密码管理器、多因素认证等安全实践的抵触。

Conclusion: 研究弥合了安全意识到行动之间的差距，为设计符合用户真实决策倾向的用户友好型认证策略提供了实践见解。

Abstract: Despite increasing awareness of cybersecurity risks, users continue to engage in insecure password practices, such as reusing passwords, choosing weak credentials, and neglecting security recommendations. The study explores the behavioral and cognitive factors that influence password decision-making by integrating insights from behavioral economics, particularly hyperbolic discounting, status quo bias, and present bias. We conducted a survey to analyze how people create, store and manage their passwords, examining whether security habits have improved over time in response to greater awareness. Our findings reveal that immediate convenience often outweighs long-term security considerations, leading users to prioritize memorability over strength. Additionally, we identify key psychological biases that contribute to security procrastination and resistance to adopting more secure authentication practices, such as password managers and multi-factor authentication. The study contributes to human-centered security by bridging the gap between security awareness and action, offering practical insights to design user-friendly authentication policies that align with real-world decision-making tendencies.

</details>


### [132] [Twin Agent: Context Residual Compression for Privilege Separated Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19595)
*Zhanhao Hu,Dennis Jacob,Xiao Huang,Zhaorun Chen,Bo Li,David Wagner*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Twin Agent模式，通过探索代理和安全代理的双代理架构实现权限分离，在保持高任务效用的同时有效防御提示注入攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理易受来自不可信上下文的提示注入攻击，现有安全设计方法虽能缓解风险但常以牺牲任务效用为代价，且需要大量任务特定的工程工作。

Method: 受残差编码启发，Twin Agent包含两个近乎对称的代理：Explore Agent检查不可信信息，Safe Agent执行特权操作。Explore Agent以Safe Agent的当前上下文为条件，仅向Safe Agent传递关于下一步行动的紧凑提示，通过控制提示长度来调节安全-效用权衡。

Result: 在SWE-bench Lite（长周期软件工程任务）、AgentDojo和DecodingTrust-Agent（异构多工具交互任务）三个基准上，Twin Agent在保持高任务效用的同时成功防御了提示注入攻击，优于未防御代理和权限分离基线方法。

Conclusion: Twin Agent通过紧凑提示设计实现了更好的安全-效用权衡，验证了在LLM代理系统中通用权限分离模式的有效性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are vulnerable to security risks, such as prompt injection attacks from untrusted context that manipulate downstream reasoning and tool use. Existing secure-by-design approaches mitigate this risk by separating untrusted observations from privileged execution and careful control of information flow, but often degrade utility and require extensive task-specific engineering. We thus propose Twin Agent, a general privilege separation design pattern inspired by residual coding in the agent context. Twin Agent consists of two nearly symmetric agents: an Explore Agent that inspects untrusted information and a Safe Agent that executes privileged actions. The Explore Agent is conditioned on the Safe Agent's current context and communicates only compact hints to the Safe Agent about the next action to take. This design reduces the information needed to preserve task utility and thus achieves a better security--utility tradeoff, which we empirically verify by measuring how utility and attack success change as the length of hints varies. We evaluate Twin Agent on long-horizon software engineering tasks with SWE-bench Lite and on heterogeneous multi-tool interaction tasks with AgentDojo and DecodingTrust-Agent. Across both benchmarks, Twin Agent preserves high task utility while preventing prompt injection attacks, outperforming both undefended agents and privilege separation baselines.

</details>


### [133] [FedLSG: LLM-Enhanced Semantic Calibration for Federated Graph Backdoor Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19674)
*Chenyu Zhou,Yabin Peng,Wei Huang,Kunlin Li,Shuaishuai Zhang,Xinyuan Miao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FedLSG是首个将大语言模型集成到联邦图神经网络后门防御中的框架，通过图和行为到文本的转换、师生架构（服务端LLM教师+客户端LoRA学生），实现对后门攻击的语义级防御，显著提升抗攻击能力且不破坏图结构完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦图神经网络（FedGNNs）极易受到后门投毒攻击，但现有防御方法多基于规则，缺乏语义理解能力，无法有效检测隐蔽触发器，且容易破坏良性图结构。

Method: 提出FedLSG框架，包含：(1) 图和行为到文本的语义转换机制，将局部图结构和客户端更新行为转化为自然语言表示；(2) 轻量级师生架构，服务端全量LLM作为教师提供全局上下文指导并评估客户端更新，客户端LoRA学生执行语义推理以抑制后门触发器相关边的影响；(3) 自适应地将规则信号融入消息传递和客户端聚合。

Result: 实验表明FedLSG显著提升了对后门攻击的抵抗能力，同时不损害图结构的完整性。

Conclusion: 通过引入LLM的语义理解能力，FedLSG有效弥补了传统规则型防御在语义层面的不足，为联邦图神经网络后门防御提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Federated Graph Neural Networks (FedGNNs) are highly vulnerable to backdoor poisoning, yet existing defenses typically rely on rule-based approaches that lack semantic understanding, making them vulnerable to stealthy triggers and harmful to benign structures. To solve this, we present FedLSG, the first framework that integrates large language models (LLMs) into federated graph backdoor defense. FedLSG introduces a graph and behavior to text grounding scheme that transforms local graph structures and client update behaviors into semantically rich natural language representations. The framework further adopts a lightweight student-teacher architecture. On the server side, a full scale LLM serves as a teacher, providing global contextual guidance and evaluating client updates during aggregation to identify potentially malicious participants. On the client side, a LoRA-based student is maintained to perform semantic reasoning, to suppress the influence of edges associated with backdoor triggers. By enabling semantic interpretation of both graph patterns and client behaviors, the framework adaptively incorporates rule-based signals into message passing and client aggregation for defense. Experiments demonstrate that FedLSG significantly improves resistance to backdoor attacks without compromising graph integrity.

</details>


### [134] [GhostPrompt: Cross-Image Adversarial Prompt for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19683)
*Li Zeng,Zeyu Ye,Meng Xie,Hangtao Zhang,Xianlong Wang,Yanchun Li,Zhetao Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出GhostPrompt，一种跨图像可迁移的对抗性提示方法。通过联合优化将图像不变对抗特征蒸馏到提示中，在主流视觉-语言模型上攻击成功率提升超30%，计算时间减少约70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本对抗攻击大多从语言模型中心方法迁移而来，优化过程中视觉输入固定，导致对抗提示与特定图像绑定，跨图像迁移性差，限制了攻击效果。

Method: 提出GhostPrompt，采用联合优化策略，交替进行"最坏情况"生成：构建当前提示下的困难视觉条件，然后更新提示使其在这些条件下仍保持有效性，从而将图像不变对抗特征蒸馏到提示中。

Result: 在主流视觉-语言模型上，相比最先进基线方法，攻击成功率提升超过30%，计算时间减少约70%。

Conclusion: GhostPrompt通过一次优化即可获得跨图像可复用的对抗提示，大幅提升了攻击效率和迁移性，为VLM对抗攻击提供了新视角。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are known to be vulnerable to adversarial attacks, where subtle perturbations to images or texts induce erroneous outputs. However, most text-based attacks are adapted from language-model-centric methods, in which the visual input is fixed during optimization, resulting in adversarial prompts that are tied to specific images and thus limiting their attack effectiveness. To this end, we first introduce a new research perspective: cross-image transferability for adversarial prompts. We then propose GhostPrompt, an adversarial prompt that is optimized once and reused to steer VLM outputs toward attacker-specified responses across diverse images. GhostPrompt employs a joint optimization that distills image-invariant adversarial features into the prompt by "worst-case" generation. Specifically, it alternates between constructing hard visual conditions for the current prompt and updating the prompt to remain effective under these conditions. Extensive experiments on prevalent VLMs verify that \ourmethod achieves an improvement of over 30% in attack success rates compared to state-of-the-art (SoTA) baselines, while reducing computation time by ~70%. Our code is avalable at https://github.com/Ye-ze-yu/GhostPrompt.

</details>


### [135] [An Automated Framework for Extracting Reachable Attack Chains from Cyber Threat Intelligence Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19742)
*Wenbo Hou,Ning Hu,Xueping Wang,Jiahao Gu,Wenjian Luo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种自动化框架，利用大语言模型从CTI报告中提取可到达的攻击链：将每个攻击步骤建模为前置条件、攻击行为和后置条件的攻击单元，并编译为Datalog规则进行攻击目标可达性推理。在20份报告、334个人工标注步骤的数据集上，攻击行为覆盖率和单元完整性均优于现有方法，19/20的报告能推理出攻击目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CTI报告包含丰富的攻击过程描述，但非结构化叙事无法直接用于自动攻击路径推理；现有提取方法聚焦于指标、实体或TTP标签，未建模每个攻击步骤的执行条件和结果状态，导致提取的知识不支持状态匹配和多阶段攻击链的可达性分析。

Method: 提出自动化框架，将每个攻击步骤建模为前置条件、攻击行为和后置条件组成的攻击单元。采用多阶段LLM辅助流水线：提取攻击行为骨架、恢复前后置条件、归一化为预定义谓词、修复断裂依赖关系；最终将攻击单元编译为Datalog规则用于可达性推理。

Result: 在20份CTI报告（334个人工验证标注步骤）上，框架在攻击行为恢复方面取得了比代表性CTI提取系统更高的标注步骤覆盖率；显式生成的前后置条件比端到端LLM基线更完整和一致；Datalog推理在19/20报告中达到指定攻击目标，后向搜索生成34条攻击路径。

Conclusion: 将攻击步骤显式建模为前置条件、行为和后置条件的攻击单元，结合LLM辅助提取与Datalog规则推理，能有效从非结构化CTI文本中构建可到达的攻击链，为自动化攻击路径推理提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Cyber Threat Intelligence (CTI) reports richly describe real-world attack processes, but their unstructured narratives cannot be directly used for automated attack-path reasoning. Existing CTI extraction methods focus on indicators, entities, or TTP labels without modeling the execution conditions and resulting states of each attack step, so the extracted knowledge supports neither state matching nor reachability analysis across multi-stage attack chains. This paper proposes an automated framework that extracts reachable attack chains by modeling each attack step as an attack unit of preconditions, an attack behavior, and postconditions. A multi-stage pipeline assisted by large language models (LLMs) extracts attack behavior skeletons, recovers their preconditions and postconditions, normalizes them into predefined predicates, and repairs broken dependencies; the resulting units are compiled into Datalog-style rules for attack-goal reachability reasoning. On a dataset of 20 CTI reports containing 334 human-validated annotated steps, our framework achieves higher annotated-step coverage than representative CTI extraction systems in recovering attack behaviors. Moreover, by explicitly generating preconditions and postconditions, it produces attack units that are more complete and consistent than those generated by end-to-end LLM baselines. On the extracted chains, Datalog inference reaches the specified attack goal in 19 of 20 reports, while backward search yields 34 attack paths under the generated rules. The source code and experimental artifacts are available in an anonymized repository. .

</details>


### [136] [DARWIN: Evolving Jailbreak Adversary and Guardrail for LLM Safety Evaluation and Protection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19829)
*Weiwei Qi,Zefeng Wu,Zhilin Guo,Tianhang Zheng,Chaochao Lu,Liang He,Zhan Qin,Kui Ren*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: DARWIN 是一个进化式攻防框架，将越狱攻击建模为开放式进化过程，通过DARWIN-Attack（进化攻击者）和DARWIN-Guard（在线对抗训练防御）的持续攻防循环，在前沿模型上实现近100%攻击成功率，同时防御达到91.6%不安全召回率且保持近100%良性样本通过率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全评估与防御方法采用静态范式（固定攻击方法评估、固定恶意数据集训练护栏），无法应对真实世界中对手不断进化攻击能力的挑战，亟需一种能持续适应演进攻防环境的动态框架。

Method: 提出DARWIN攻防框架：(1) DARWIN-Attack：进化式攻击者，通过外部策略收集、自我反思与遗传进化进行策略发现与变异，根据反馈自适应选择组合策略；(2) DARWIN-Guard：在线对抗训练范式，从攻击产生的对抗样本中迭代学习，同时训练恶意与良性伪装查询以识别底层意图而非表面模式。

Result: 攻击方面，在前沿模型和护栏上达到SOTA攻击成功率，包括DeepSeek-V4-Pro和YuFeng-XGuard近100%，GPT-5.5超90%；防御方面，在12个安全基准上平均不安全召回率91.6%，超越YuFeng-XGuard和Nemotron Guard，同时在标准良性数据集上保持近100%通过率。

Conclusion: DARWIN通过将越狱攻击与防御建模为开放式进化过程，有效弥合了静态安全范式与动态真实世界威胁之间的差距，证明了持续演化的攻防循环是提升LLM安全的有效路径。

Abstract: Most existing LLM safety evaluation and defense methods follow a static formulation: jailbreak vulnerabilities are evaluated with fixed attack methods, and guardrails are trained on fixed malicious prompt datasets. However, real-world adversaries continuously evolve their capabilities and expand the attack space. To address this challenge, we propose DARWIN, an evolutionary attack-defense framework that formulates jailbreaking as an open-ended evolution process and continuously updates guardrails through an evolving attack-defense loop. DARWIN-Attack is an evolutionary adversary that expands its capabilities through strategy discovery, mutation, selection, and feedback-driven composition. It collects strategies from broad external sources, generates new variants through self-reflection and genetic evolution, and retains effective strategies based on their performance against aligned LLMs. During attack execution, DARWIN-Attack adaptively selects and combines evolved strategies according to feedback from target LLMs and guardrails. Across frontier models and guardrails, it achieves state-of-the-art attack success rates, including nearly 100% on DeepSeek-V4-Pro and YuFeng-XGuard and over 90% on GPT-5.5. On the defense side, we introduce DARWIN-Guard, an online adversarial training paradigm that iteratively learns from emerging adversarial samples generated by DARWIN-Attack. To improve robustness without sacrificing utility, DARWIN-Guard jointly trains on malicious and benign disguised queries, encouraging the model to identify underlying intent rather than superficial attack patterns. DARWIN-Guard achieves an average unsafe recall of 91.6% across 12 safety benchmarks, outperforming strong guardrails such as YuFeng-XGuard and Nemotron Guard, while maintaining a nearly 100% pass rate on standard benign datasets.

</details>


### [137] [Defense Against LLM Backdoors using Critical Neuron Isolation Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19894)
*Yuxi Li,Zhibo Zhang,Kailong Wang,Xingshuo Han,Ling Shi,Haoyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出DeCNIP方法，通过表征分析识别并修剪后门关键神经元，在仅干预0.1%神经元的情况下将攻击成功率降低95%以上，同时保持模型97%的正常性能，统一解决了微调后门和模型编辑攻击下的LLM安全问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御方法存在两大局限：一是在于仅针对微调后门（如PEFT模块），无法应对绕过训练流程的模型编辑攻击；二是仅适用于简单分类任务，无法自然扩展到LLM的开放式生成场景。这些方法依赖表层行为模式的经验启发式，缺乏对恶意激活深层表征机制的机理理解。

Method: DeCNIP包含两个核心阶段：1) 表征发现阶段：通过优化有害提示与候选令牌及良性输入之间的交叉熵损失，识别触发式行为，揭示触发器劫持模型权重的潜在机制；2) 神经元隔离与修剪阶段：识别后门关键神经元（BCNs），选择性修剪以消除恶意影响，同时保留模型效用。

Result: 在6个开源LLM和2个基准数据集上的评估表明，DeCNIP实现95%以上的攻击成功率相对降低，仅需0.1%的神经元干预即可超越7种现有防御方法，并在常规基准测试中保持模型97%的性能。

Conclusion: DeCNIP通过基于表征分析的后门关键神经元隔离修剪，提供了一种统一、鲁棒且可扩展的LLM后门防御方案，弥补了现有方法在机理理解和通用性上的不足。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are vulnerable to backdoor attacks, where hidden triggers induce malicious outputs. Existing defenses generally fall into inference-time detection or training-time mitigation, but face two key limitations. First, they focus on fine-tuning-based backdoors (e.g., PEFT modules) and fail to address insidious model-editing attacks that bypass training pipelines. Second, they target simple classification settings and do not naturally extend to open-ended LLM generation and do not naturally extend to the open-ended generation characteristics of LLMs. Consequently, these methods focus on surface-level behavioral patterns while neglecting the deeper representational causes of malicious activations. This lack of mechanistic understanding forces defenses to depend on empirical heuristics, limiting their robustness, generality, and practical applicability in real-world LLM deployment.
  To bridge this gap, we introduce DeCNIP (Defense with Critical Neuron Isolation Pruning), which leverages representational analysis to identify and neutralize backdoors in a unified pipeline. Specifically, DeCNIP identifies trigger-like behaviors by optimizing a cross-entropy loss between harmful prompts with candidate tokens and benign inputs. This representational discovery exposes latent threats by uncovering mechanisms through which triggers hijack model weights. It then isolates Backdoor Critical Neurons (BCNs) and prunes them selectively to remove malicious influence while preserving model utility. Extensive evaluations on six open-source LLMs and two benchmark datasets demonstrate that DeCNIP achieves over 95% relative reduction in Attack Success Rate (ASR), outperforming seven state-of-the-art defenses with only 0.1% neuron intervention. Moreover, it maintains 97% of the model's performance on normal benchmarks, demonstrating its efficacy, robustness, and scalability.

</details>


### [138] [HijackKV: New Threat in Position-Independent KV Cache Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19957)
*Yichi Zhang,Zhiqi Wang,Huan Zhang,Yuchen Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一种针对大语言模型KV缓存重用机制的新攻击——KV缓存劫持（KV Cache Hijacking），并提出了首个系统化攻击框架HIJACKKV，该攻击通过构造恶意前缀使普通文本的KV编码攻击者意图，在缓存命中时无声劫持模型行为，平均单次成功率达94%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存重用优化虽然提升了效率，但基于token匹配的检索方式使得攻击者可以通过控制前缀将恶意意图编码进共享文本的KV缓存中，导致后续无辜用户使用相同文本时模型行为被劫持。这种攻击不需要攻击者文本出现在输入中，隐蔽性极强，现有安全机制无法防御。

Method: HIJACKKV通过优化一个攻击者可控的前缀，使得该前缀与后续公共良性文本共同计算出的KV缓存能够编码攻击者的目标意图，同时保证该良性文本本身在token序列上保持不变以触发缓存命中。攻击框架考虑了多种现实约束条件下的优化策略。

Result: HIJACKKV平均单次攻击成功率达94%；在低缓存命中率（10%）和频繁重计算（50%）的现实条件下仍然有效；能够在多轮交互中持续生效；在黑盒设置下可跨模型迁移。

Conclusion: KV缓存重用机制存在严重安全漏洞，HIJACKKV证明了该攻击的严重性和实用性。作者提出了构建安全KV重用系统的设计洞察，为未来系统优化提供安全指导。

Abstract: Key-Value (KV) cache reduces inference latency in large language models (LLMs). Traditional prefix-based reuse has low cache hit rates across inference requests because it requires exact token and position matches. To improve efficiency, recent system optimizations introduce position-independent KV reuse, allowing KV cache to be reused whenever identical text chunks appear, regardless of their position in the sequence.
  We show this design introduces a new threat, KV Cache Hijacking. Since KV caches are retrieved by token match but encode the context in which they were originally computed, the KV tied to a benign-looking token chunk may encode an attacker-controlled prefix. When later reused in a victim query, this contaminated KV silently hijacks the model's behavior, even if no attacker-controlled text appears in the input.
  We introduce HIJACKKV, the first attack framework that systematically exploits this vulnerability, demonstrating its severity and practicality. HIJACKKV optimizes an attacker-controlled prefix, so that the KV computed for a subsequent common benign text encodes the attacker's goal, while the text remains unchanged for future cache hits. HIJACKKV achieves an average 94% success rate in a single attempt, remains effective under realistic constraints including low hit rates (10%) and frequent recomputation (50%), persists over multi-turn interactions, and transfers across models in black-box settings. We further provide design insights for building secure KV reuse systems.

</details>


### [139] [Taming the Security-Energy Paradox: A Green AI Approach to Optimized Android Malware Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20003)
*Shrinidhi Sridhar,Vikas K. Malviya*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了在Android设备上使用不同配置的多层感知机（MLP）模型来平衡恶意软件检测性能与能耗。INT8量化相比FP32可将模型体积缩小约3.5倍，每次推理能耗降至0.0189 mJ，同时保持99.2%以上的检测准确率。浅层量化架构（3层和4层QNN）通过提高吞吐量、缩短CPU高功耗状态运行时间，有效降低了能耗，为实现移动设备上的绿色AI安全防护奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着高级Android恶意软件增多，需要在设备端运行深度学习模型进行检测，但强检测模型会快速耗尽设备电池，存在安全性与能耗之间的权衡问题。

Method: 对比标准FP32模型与INT8量化神经网络在不同模型深度下的分类性能和能耗，使用TUANDROMD和DREBIN数据集进行实验。

Result: INT8量化将模型体积缩小约3.5倍，能耗降至0.0189 mJ/推理，检测准确率保持99.2%以上；浅层量化架构（3层和4层QNN）通过提高吞吐量、缩短CPU高功耗状态运行时间降低能耗。

Conclusion: 在资源受限的智能手机上可以实现高效的恶意软件防护，为移动安全领域的绿色AI奠定了基础。

Abstract: An increase in advanced Android malware requires the use of deep learning models, which can run on Android devices. But there is a trade-off between security and energy use, as strong detection models can drain the battery of devices fast. This work tests different Multi-Layer Perceptron (MLP) model configurations to balance malware detection performance and energy efficiency. In this work, we compared standard FP32 models with optimized INT8 quantized neural networks with different model depths using TUANDROMD and DREBIN datasets for both classification performance and energy consumption. The results show that INT8 quantization reduces model size by about 3.5 times with a decrease in energy consumption to 0.0189 mJ per inference, while maintaining more than 99.2\% detection accuracy. We found that shallow quantized architectures, such as 3-layer and 4-layer QNNs, reduce energy costs by improving throughput and shortening the time of CPU operating in a high-power state. This work shows that efficient malware protection can be achieved on resource-constrained smartphones and provides a foundation for Green AI in mobile security.

</details>


### [140] [Small, Free, and Effective: Orchestrating Open-Weight Small Language Models to Outperform Single LLM for Malware Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20216)
*Adel ElZemity,Shujun Li,Budi Arief*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究基于小型语言模型（SLM）的编排架构在恶意软件分析任务中是否能够达到或超越大型语言模型（LLM）的性能。通过在Meta的CyberSecEval Malware Analysis基准上测试11个SLM、3个网络安全预训练模型和6个前沿LLM，并设计了四种编排架构。其中混合系统（Qwen3-4B与Foundation-Sec-8B）达到35.30%的总体准确率，超过了最强的网络安全专业基线（22.54%）和最强的无基础前沿基线（34.77%），表明基于证据的编排架构能显著提升协作式SLM在恶意软件分析中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恶意软件分析需要快速解读涉及文件系统、网络和进程行为的复杂引爆报告。虽然大型语言模型（LLM）在技术工件解释方面表现优异，但闭权重前沿模型的不透明性和高昂API成本促使研究者探索开权重替代方案。然而许多开权重模型体积庞大，计算资源和托管成本高昂，超出资源受限部署的能力范围。因此需要研究能否通过编排小型语言模型（SLM）组合来匹配或超越单个LLM在恶意软件报告结构化问答任务上的性能。

Method: 首先在Meta的CyberSecEval Malware Analysis基准上测试11个开权重SLM、3个网络安全预训练模型和6个前沿LLM建立基线。然后设计并评估四种编排架构：(i)多智能体流水线，将分析分解为结构化证据收集和推理阶段；(ii)对抗性辩论框架，两个智能体迭代批评对方的推理；(iii)层次化咨询系统，通用SLM配合网络安全专家模型；(iv)混合架构，结合证据驱动流水线与对抗性辩论推理。最终混合系统采用Qwen3-4B与Foundation-Sec-8B组合。

Result: 混合系统（Qwen3-4B与Foundation-Sec-8B）达到35.30%总体准确率，超过最强网络安全专业基线（22.54%）、最强无基础前沿基线（34.77%）和所有其他编排架构。当使用相同证据流水线时，有基础的Gemini仍是最强配置（38.22%）。

Conclusion: 基于证据的编排架构能够显著提升协作式小型语言模型在恶意软件引爆报告解释任务中的表现，使资源受限的部署场景也能获得接近甚至超越前沿LLM的性能。

Abstract: Malware analysis demands rapid interpretation of complex detonation reports spanning filesystem, network, and process behaviours. While large language models (LLMs) demonstrate impressive capabilities for technical artifact interpretation, the opacity and escalating API costs of closed-weight frontier models motivate exploration of open-weight alternatives. However, many open-weight models are large, demanding significant compute resources and incurring non-trivial hosting costs that place them beyond reach for resource-constrained deployments. This paper investigates whether orchestrated ensembles of small language models (SLMs) can match or exceed single LLM performance on structured questions about malware detonation reports. We established baselines by testing eleven open-weight SLMs, three cyber security pre-trained models, and six frontier LLMs on Meta's CyberSecEval Malware Analysis benchmark. We then designed and evaluated four orchestration architectures: (i) a multi-agent pipeline that decomposes analysis into structured evidence-collection and reasoning stages, (ii) an adversarial debate framework in which two agents iteratively critique each other's reasoning, (iii) a hierarchical consultation system that pairs a general-purpose SLM with a cyber-specialised expert model, and (iv) a hybrid architecture that combines evidence-grounded pipelines with adversarial debate reasoning. The hybrid system (Qwen3-4B with Foundation-Sec-8B) achieved 35.30% overall accuracy, exceeding the strongest cyber-specialised baseline (22.54%) and the strongest ungrounded frontier baseline (34.77%); when given the same evidence pipeline, grounded Gemini remained the strongest configuration at 38.22%. These findings show that evidence-grounded orchestration can substantially improve the performance of collaborative SLMs for supporting interpretation of malware detonation reports.

</details>


### [141] [The Ethics of Autonomous AI Agents for Offensive Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20255)
*Andreas Happe,Jürgen Cito,Jasmin Wachter*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文分析了LLM驱动的自主智能体在 offensive security 中引入的三重不确定性（动作、影响、用户群体），指出这些特性结合攻防成本不对称将工业化攻击能力，并探讨了现有框架在道德归因和利益相关者影响方面的不足，提出了分层建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统渗透测试工具是确定性的、范围狭窄且需专业人员操作，而LLM驱动的自主智能体引入了全新的不确定性维度，现有AI伦理和网络安全框架无法应对这种组合，亟需分析其道德归因和利益相关者影响。

Method: 通过理论分析，识别并定义LLM自主智能体在 offensive security 中的三个不确定性维度（动作不确定性、影响不确定性、用户群体不确定性），分析攻防成本不对称的叠加效应，并基于此进行道德归因分析和利益相关者影响评估。

Result: 三重不确定性使攻击能力工业化成为可能，短期有利于攻击者；现有双用途网络安全和AI伦理框架未针对此组合设计；道德责任在用户、工具制造者和第三方之间变得分散。

Conclusion: 需重新审视现有框架以应对LLM自主智能体在 offensive security 中带来的独特挑战，并提供分层建议以管理风险。

Abstract: LLM-driven autonomous agents are reshaping offensive security. Unlike traditional penetration-testing tooling -- deterministic, narrowly scoped, and operated by trained practitioners -- agentic security tools exhibit \textit{indeterminacy} along three independent dimensions. First, their actions are drawn from a non-deterministic policy whose outputs resist both ex-ante and ex-post explanation, frustrating incident attribution and pre-deployment safety review. Second, their impact is open-ended due to the non-deterministic actions, agency of utilized models, and opaque LLM supply-chains. Third, their user population is indeterminate in both size and required skill: the operating skill floor for using or developing offensive capabilities has dropped sharply. These three properties are linked thematically, but are not derivable from one another. Combined with the structural cost asymmetry between offense and defense, they enable the industrialization of offensive capability. The net short-term effect favors attackers, even if the same technology may, in the long run, democratize access to defensive practice. Existing dual-use cybersecurity and AI-ethics frameworks were not designed for this combination. Our work analyzes how moral attribution becomes diffuse between users, tool-makers, and third parties when employing autonomous AI agents for offensive security. We also examine the stakeholder impact of this technology and provide stratified recommendations.

</details>


### [142] [Chained Attacks on Drone-Based Federated Learning: From Network Disruption to Device Impersonation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20280)
*Suleiman Muhammad Sabo,Hamed Alkharsh,Peilin Li,Chuadhry Mujeeb Ahmed,Aydin Abadi,Shishir Nagaraja,Rajiv Ranjan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了无人机群联邦学习系统中结合网络层去认证攻击与凭证窃取的链式攻击，通过实证验证了短期无线中断会导致训练不稳定（尤其非IID数据下），且单因素认证无法防止身份冒用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘智能在无人机群等关键任务平台中广泛应用，但联邦学习部署的安全性同时依赖于网络可用性和客户端认证机制，现有研究缺乏对这两类漏洞联合利用的链式攻击分析。

Method: 通过系统性文献综述和基于Flower框架的实证验证，在Raspberry Pi和Jetson两种测试平台上，对IID和非IID数据分布下的可用性中断影响进行量化分析，并验证单因素认证下的身份冒用可行性。

Result: 短期无线中断会引发显著的训练不稳定性，尤其非IID条件下影响更为严重；单因素认证机制无法阻止攻击者在节点断连后实施身份冒用。

Conclusion: 指出了关键任务无人机部署中可用性与认证双重漏洞的复合影响，并提出了未来防御方向。

Abstract: Edge Intelligence (EI) has emerged as a transformative model for mission-critical unmanned platforms, such as drone swarms, by enabling collaborative model training at the network periphery. However, the security of FL deployments depends on both network availability and robust client authentication mechanisms. This paper investigates a chained attack against drone-based FL systems that combines network-layer denial-of-service with credential-based impersonation. We demonstrate that an adversary can: (1) force legitimate drones offline using 802.11 deauthentication attacks, and (2) subsequently impersonate the disconnected drone using extracted credentials. Through a systematic literature review and empirical validation using the Flower framework on two distinct testbeds of Raspberry Pi and Jetsons, we quantify the impact of availability disruptions under Independent and Identically Distributed (IID) and Non-Independently and Identically Distributed (Non-IID) data distributions, and confirm that single-factor authentication permits post-disconnect impersonation. Our findings reveal that even short-term wireless interruptions cascade into substantial training instability, particularly under non-IID conditions, while the authentication gap enables adversaries to seamlessly replace disconnected nodes. We discuss the compounded implications for mission-critical drone deployments and outline directions for future defenses addressing both availability and authentication vulnerabilities.

</details>


### [143] [Constant-time decoding of Gabidulin codes and their generalizations with application to RQC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20305)
*Nicolas Aragon,Chloé Baïsse,Anthony Fraga,Philippe Gaborit,Ilaria Zappatore*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次提出了增广Gabidulin码的恒定时间译码算法（包含经典Gabidulin码作为特例），实现了二次复杂度，并将其集成到RQC密码系统中，在性能与紧凑性之间取得了良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gabidulin码广泛应用于基于秩度量的密码系统（如RQC、Loidreau），但缺乏恒定时间实现，而这对于密码系统的实际开发至关重要。

Method: 提出了增广Gabidulin码的恒定时间译码算法，复杂度为二次；设计了q-多项式左除的恒定时间算法；完整描述了AG码译码流程；并将其集成到RQC-Block-MS-AG方案中。

Result: 实现优于原始RQC，但比HQC慢约4倍；密文和密钥大小约为HQC的1/4，展现了性能与紧凑性之间的良好权衡。

Conclusion: 本文填补了Gabidulin码恒定时间实现的空白，证明了AG码译码可达到二次复杂度，为基于秩度量的密码系统提供了实用的高效实现方案。

Abstract: Gabidulin codes are a rank metric analog of Reed-Solomon codes. Although these codes are used in different very efficient rank-based cryptosystems like the RQC cryptosystem or the Loidreau cryptosystem, there was no constant-time implementation of Gabidulin codes, when having a constant-time implementation is crucial for real-life development of cryptosystems. In this paper, we propose the first constant-time decoding algorithm of Augmented Gabidulin (AG) codes, a simple variation on Gabidulin codes where one adds zero columns to Gabidulin codes, and which contains the case of Gabidulin codes. These AG codes are used in practice in the most efficient variations of the RQC cryptosystem. We prove that AG code decoding can be achieved with quadratic complexity. We further present a constant-time algorithm for the left division of $q$-polynomials along with a complete description of the AG code decoding procedure. These algorithms are integrated into the RQC-Block-MS-AG scheme, and we evaluate the performance of our implementation through benchmarks. Our results show that our implementation outperforms the original RQC, though it remains approximately four times slower than HQC. However, it achieves ciphertexts and key sizes about four times smaller, highlighting an appealing trade-off between performance and compactness.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [144] [Statistical Inference for Rank Allocation in Low-Rank Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20205)
*Yihang Gao,Vincent Y. F. Tan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: StatLoRA提出了一种基于统计假设检验的LoRA秩分配方法，利用优化器轨迹的渐近正态性理论，通过p值决定保留或剪枝LoRA组件，在固定参数预算下实现更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应秩分配方法主要依赖基于梯度导出的敏感性/不确定性度量构建重要性分数，缺乏显式的统计解释，且在不同模块和层间合理分配秩资源以平衡效率、表达能力和泛化性的问题尚未得到充分解决。

Method: 将LoRA秩分配形式化为统计假设检验问题，为每个LoRA组件关联一个检验统计量，利用估计的p值在给定秩预算下决定组件的保留或剪枝；建立了AdamW等广泛使用的深度学习优化器轨迹的渐近正态性理论，并推导了所提出组件分数的渐近分布。

Result: 在DeBERTaV3-base、BART-Large和Qwen2.5-7B上，针对自然语言理解、生成和问答任务，StatLoRA在匹配秩预算下达到了与vanilla LoRA、AdaLoRA和IGU-LoRA相当或更优的性能。

Conclusion: 基于统计假设检验的秩分配方法有效且稳定，敏感性分析和经验诊断为所提出的渐近理论提供了实证支持。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) has become a widely used parameter-efficient fine-tuning method for large language models. Since different modules and layers may contribute unequally to downstream adaptation, allocating rank resources under a fixed parameter budget is an important problem for balancing efficiency, expressiveness, and generalization. Existing adaptive rank methods address this problem mainly through carefully designed importance scores constructed from gradient-derived sensitivity and uncertainty measures, without an explicit statistical interpretation. In this paper, we formulate LoRA rank allocation as a statistical hypothesis testing problem and propose StatLoRA, a statistical inference-based rank allocation method. StatLoRA associates each LoRA component with a test statistic and uses estimated p-values to determine which components should be retained or pruned under a prescribed rank budget. The proposed testing procedure is supported by our central limit theory for stochastic optimizer trajectories. In particular, we establish asymptotic normality for a broad class of commonly used optimizers in deep learning, including AdamW, and derive the corresponding asymptotic distributions for the proposed component scores used in hypothesis testing. We evaluate StatLoRA on LoRA fine-tuning of DeBERTaV3-base, BART-Large, and Qwen2.5-7B across natural language understanding, natural language generation, and question answering tasks. Experiments show that StatLoRA achieves comparable or better performance than vanilla LoRA, AdaLoRA, and IGU-LoRA under matched rank budgets. Sensitivity analyses and empirical diagnostics further support the stability of the proposed hypothesis-testing-based allocation rule and provide empirical evidence for the asymptotic theory of component scores.

</details>


### [145] [A Bayesian Framework for Built-in Input Dimension Reduction for Gaussian Process Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19498)
*Eric Herrison Gyamfi,Emily L. Kang,Bledar A. Konomi,Guang Lin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种贝叶斯框架，将高斯过程建模与降维无缝集成，通过在Stiefel流形上设置先验并使用哈密顿蒙特卡洛进行后验推断，同时扩展到深度高斯过程，在提高预测性能和不确定性量化方面优于传统两阶段方法，尽管计算成本更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维输入下的高斯过程建模面临维度灾难，现有的降维方法通常采用两阶段策略（先降维再拟合GP），这种分离方式可能导致信息损失和次优解。作者希望开发一种能够将降维与GP建模统一进行的框架。

Method: 构建层次贝叶斯模型，在Stiefel流形上为正交投影矩阵设置先验，利用哈密顿蒙特卡洛（HMC）结合测地流进行后验推断；进一步将该框架扩展到深度高斯过程（DGP），实现内置降维功能。

Result: 广泛的数值研究表明，所提出的贝叶斯方法虽然计算成本更高，但在预测性能和不确定性量化方面优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 该方法为高维GP建模提供了一种原则性且鲁棒的替代方案，能够更有效地处理高维复杂数据集。

Abstract: Gaussian process (GP) modeling is widely used in computational science and engineering. However, fitting a GP to high-dimensional inputs remains challenging due to the curse of dimensionality. While various methods have been proposed to reduce input dimensionality, they typically follow a two-stage approach, performing dimension reduction and GP fitting separately. We introduce a Bayesian framework that seamlessly integrates dimensionality reduction with GP modeling and inference. Our approach, built on a hierarchical Bayesian model with priors on the Stiefel manifold, enforces orthonormality on the projection matrix and enables posterior inference via Hamiltonian Monte Carlo with geodesic flow. Additionally, we extend this framework by incorporating Deep Gaussian Processes (DGP) with built-in dimension reduction, providing a more flexible and powerful tool for complex datasets. Through extensive numerical studies, we demonstrate that while the proposed Bayesian method incurs higher computational costs, it improves predictive performance and uncertainty quantification, providing a principled and robust alternative to existing methods.

</details>


### [146] [Boltzmann-Expected Molecular Design with Decoupled Annealing Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19519)
*Selma Moqvist,Richard Beckmann,Ross Irwin,Rocío Mercado,Simon Olsson*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: DECAF（解耦退火流）将3D分子设计重新定义为玻尔兹曼期望设计，通过两个条件流模型交替优化分子图，以系综平均而非单构象属性为目标，实现系综感知的分子优化，支持多目标权衡和高阶矩设计（如方差和偏度），并在GEOM-Drugs数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有属性引导的3D生成模型将每个属性绑定到单一结构，忽略了分子在玻尔兹曼分布下的构象系综，导致对大而柔性的药物分子优化效果不佳。

Method: 将3D分子设计重新定义为玻尔兹曼期望设计，提出DECAF框架：分解联合分布为两个条件流模型——图条件流p(x|G)作为玻尔兹曼模拟器生成构象系综，坐标条件流p(G|x)从3D信息提议新图；通过模拟退火接受规则交替优化，评分函数基于系综统计而非单构象。

Result: 在GEOM-Drugs上，系综感知优化使分子均方回转半径和溶剂可及表面积稳定向目标偏移，而单构象优化在大药物分子上性能退化；DECAF还实现了多目标权衡和高阶矩设计（优化系综属性的方差和偏度），并通过全原子MD模拟验证了构象分布。

Conclusion: DECAF证明了系综感知的分子设计是可行且更符合物理现实的范式，能够处理柔性药物分子的宽玻尔兹曼分布，并拓展到传统的单构象方法无法触及的高阶矩设计。

Abstract: Most 3D properties relevant to molecular design, including free energies and shape descriptors, are $\textit{expectations}$ over the Boltzmann distribution over 3D configurations of a molecular graph. However, existing property-guided generative models tie each property to a single structure, ignoring the underlying ensemble. We recast 3D molecular design as $\textbf{Boltzmann-expected design}$ and realise it with $\textbf{DECAF}$ (Decoupled Annealing Flows), which factorise the joint distribution over graphs and coordinates into two conditional flow models: a graph-conditioned flow $p(x\mid\mathcal{G})$, acting as a $\textit{Boltzmann emulator}$, and a coordinate-conditioned flow $p(\mathcal{G}\mid x)$, proposing new graphs from 3D information. By alternating the two flows, DECAF optimises molecular graphs with a simulated-annealing acceptance rule whose scoring function is evaluated on ensembles drawn from $p(x\mid\mathcal{G})$, making ensemble statistics, not single-conformer properties, the design target. The resulting loop requires no retraining to change objectives. On GEOM-Drugs, we show that ensemble-aware optimisation produces graphs whose mean radius of gyration and solvent-accessible surface area consistently shift toward targets, while single-conformer optimisation degrades on larger drug-like molecules where Boltzmann distributions are broadest. DECAF extends to multi-objective trade-offs and, uniquely among 3D generative models, to $\textbf{higher-moment design}$: jointly optimising an ensemble property's variance and skewness to produce flexible molecules biased to a prescribed conformational regime: we verify the conformational distributions of these higher-moment designs with all-atom MD simulations.

</details>


### [147] [RELTA-SGLD: Relative-Growth Localized Taming for Nonconvex Stochastic-Gradient Langevin Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19544)
*Yiwei Zhou,Ziheng Chen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出RELTA-SGLD，一种通过相对增长原则和阈值机制来稳定超线性随机梯度更新的方法，在理论上证明了多项式矩稳定性和一阶驻点精度，实验上在Fashion-MNIST上优于未驯化SGLD和TUSLA，并与调优的AdamW竞争力相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超线性增长的随机梯度或acles会导致SGLD不稳定，需要有效的驯化方案来平衡稳定性和原始学习漂移的保留。

Method: 设计阈值确定驯化激活时机，基于一步Lyapunov稳定性条件推导相对增长原则以确定所需驯化强度，产生更轻的λ尺度分母并保留非零远尾回报。

Result: 在非凸SGLD中证明了多项式矩稳定性和W1及W2上的一阶驻点精度，分别比同类随机梯度驯化方案提高了半阶和四分之一阶的界；在Fashion-MNIST上，主动稳定压力下优于未驯化SGLD和TUSLA，常规训练中几乎保持未驯化学习动态。

Conclusion: RELTA-SGLD有效解决了超线性梯度下的稳定性问题，同时最小化了对原始学习过程的干扰，在理论和实验上均优于现有驯化方案。

Abstract: We introduce RELTA-SGLD, a taming scheme that stabilizes superlinear stochastic-gradient updates while reducing unnecessary suppression of the original learning drift. A threshold determines where the taming turns on, while a relative-growth principle derived from the one-step Lyapunov stability condition determines the required taming strength. Together, they produce a lighter $λ$-scale denominator and preserve a nonvanishing far-tail return. As a consequence, we prove polynomial moment stability and first-order stationary accuracy in both $W_1$ and $W_2$ for nonconvex SGLD with superlinearly growing stochastic-gradient oracles, improving the corresponding half-order and quarter-order bounds for comparable stochastic-gradient tamed schemes. On Fashion-MNIST under active stabilization pressure, RELTA improves the mean learning metrics over both untamed SGLD and TUSLA and remains competitive with a tuned AdamW reference. In an ordinary-training regime, its lighter localized denominator reduces unnecessary perturbation of the original update and maintains nearly untamed learning dynamics.

</details>


### [148] [Optimal Recalibration of an Online Predictor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19689)
*Lunjia Hu,Kevin Tian,Chutong Yang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究在线预测器的重新校准问题，提出了一种基于非平衡Blackwell可接近性的在线算法，在T≈ε⁻³轮内实现(ε, ε²)-重新校准（对Lipschitz proper损失最优），并应用于同时实现ε-校准和ε²-calibeating，改进了先前工作的依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线预测器可能输出未校准的预测，需要一种方法在保持预测精度的同时进行重新校准，而此前缺乏最优的重新校准算法及其与calibeating的联合保证。

Method: 1) 提出非平衡扩展的同步Blackwell可接近性约简框架；2) 设计在线算法实现(ε, ε²)-重新校准；3) 结合[FH23]的在线精炼方法同时实现校准和calibeating；4) 推导多提示序列的扩展。

Result: 1) 对Lipschitz proper损失在T≈ε⁻³轮内达到(ε, ε²)-重新校准，且对平方损失最优；2) K₂变体达到对数因子内的相同权衡；3) 同时实现ε-校准和ε²-calibeating，优于先前工作；4) 在分类数据集上验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 本文建立了重新校准的最优权衡，并展示了如何利用此结果同时改进校准与calibeating，解决了[CHJL26]提出的开放问题。

Abstract: We study the problem of recalibrating an online predictor [KE17, OKS24]: given an arbitrary "hint" sequence of forecasts, the learner must output new predictions that are calibrated while incurring small excess error relative to the original forecasts, under a proper loss. We give an online algorithm that achieves $(\varepsilon, \varepsilon^2)$-recalibration for Lipschitz proper losses in
  $T \approx \varepsilon^{-3}$ rounds, using an imbalanced extension of the recent simultaneous Blackwell approachability reduction framework of [HTY26]. We show that this tradeoff is optimal by proving a matching lower bound for recalibrating against the squared loss. We also prove a companion $\mathcal{K}_2$-recalibration theorem that obtains the same tradeoffs up to a logarithmic factor.
  As our main application, we show how our recalibration algorithms can be combined with the online refinement method of [FH23] to obtain simultaneous $\varepsilon$-calibration and $\varepsilon^2$-calibeating for smooth proper losses at the same asymptotic rate, improving upon prior works that achieved these properties separately or with a worse $\varepsilon$ dependence. In particular, the $\mathcal{K}_2$ variant answers a question of [CHJL26] on simultaneously achieving near-optimal calibeating and calibration rates. We also derive extensions to settings with multiple hint sequences. Finally, we empirically evaluate our algorithms on a classification dataset undergoing distribution shift.

</details>


### [149] [Data-Poisoning Audits for Causal Effect Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19692)
*Kwangho Kim*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对观测性因果分析中因跨站点、供应商和系统整合数据而易受"仅追加攻击"（通过策略性选择看似合理的记录来改变报告的治疗效果）的问题，开发了一个数据投毒审计框架。该框架基于增强型逆概率加权估计，通过贪心扫描算法精确计算固定预算下的最坏情况偏移，并提出总影响分数来考虑 nuisance 重拟合效应，最终通过移动曲线和临界预算支持更可靠的因果报告和源头级防护设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观测性因果分析越来越多地跨站点、供应商和系统整合数据，这产生了对"仅追加攻击"（append-only attack）的脆弱性——即攻击者通过策略性地选择看似合理的虚假记录来操纵报告的治疗效果。现有方法缺乏对这种数据投毒风险的系统性审计手段。

Method: 提出一个数据投毒审计框架：1）定义有限可行记录目录、追加预算和嵌套源容量；2）对增强型逆概率加权（AIPW）估计，在预处理和 nuisance 拟合固定时，提出贪心扫描算法精确计算每个追加预算下的最坏情况偏移；3）推导总影响分数，结合每条记录的直接贡献及其通过倾向得分和结果模型产生的间接效应；4）获得完全重拟合估计的保守有限预算上界。

Result: 大量模拟验证了精确结果的有效性，并表明总影响分数改进了局部重拟合预测。多站点和公开数据分析展示了在较小的追加预算下材料敏感性。实验证明该框架能够有效将对抗性数据组合风险转化为移动曲线和临界预算。

Conclusion: 通过将对抗性数据组合风险量化为移动曲线和临界预算，该框架支持更可靠的因果报告和源头级防护设计，为观测性因果分析中的安全性提供了系统性的审计方法。

Abstract: Observational causal analyses increasingly pool records across sites, vendors, and collection systems, creating vulnerability to append-only attacks in which plausible records are strategically selected to alter a reported treatment effect. We develop a data-poisoning audit for augmented inverse-probability-weighted estimation. The analyst specifies a finite catalog of feasible records, an append budget, and nested source capacities, and the adversary selects a feasible subset to maximize movement in a prespecified direction. With preprocessing and nuisance fits held fixed, we propose a greedy scan that computes the exact finite-sample worst-case movement at every append budget. To account for nuisance refitting, we go on to derive a total-influence score combining each record's direct contribution with its effect through the propensity and outcome models. We further obtain a conservative finite-budget bound for the fully refitted estimate. Extensive simulations validate the exact result and show that total influence improves local refit prediction, while multisite and public-data analyses demonstrate material sensitivity at small append budgets. By translating adversarial data-composition risk into movement curves and critical budgets, the framework supports more reliable causal reporting and the design of source-level safeguards.

</details>


### [150] [Non--negative matrix factorization using the \textit{R} package \textsf{nnmf}](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20084)
*Volkan Sevinç,Nikolas Kontemeniotis,Theodoros Perdikis,Michail Tsagris*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个新的R语言NMF包，并在真实数据集上系统比较了它与两个主流R包在计算效率、收敛性、重构精度、内存使用和分解稳定性方面的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管NMF在多个领域广泛应用并有多个R包实现，但缺乏基于真实数据条件的全面评估，导致研究者选择包时缺乏客观依据。

Method: 引入一个新的R NMF包，并在真实数据（而非模拟数据）上与两个主流R包进行系统比较，采用统一的实验框架评估多个性能指标。

Result: 在计算效率、收敛行为、重构精度、内存利用率和分解稳定性等维度上给出了各包的性能对比结果。

Conclusion: 该研究为研究者选择适合实际应用的NMF R包提供了客观的实证指导。

Abstract: Non--negative matrix factorization (NMF) has become an established dimensionality reduction technique for extracting latent structures from non--negative data and has found widespread applications in fields such as bioinformatics, text mining, image analysis, and recommender systems. As the popularity of NMF has increased, numerous \textit{R} packages implementing different optimization strategies and computational frameworks have been developed. Despite their widespread availability, comprehensive evaluations of these implementations under real--world data conditions remain limited. Consequently, researchers often lack objective guidance when selecting an appropriate package for practical applications. This study introduces a new \textit{R} package for NMF and offers asystematic performance comparison with two widely available \textit{R} packages for NMF analysis. Rather than relying on simulated datasets, the evaluation is conducted using real--world data to better reflect the complexity, heterogeneity, and noise characteristics encountered in practical analytical settings. The packages are assessed using a consistent experimental framework, with emphasis on computational efficiency, convergence behavior, reconstruction accuracy, memory utilization, and the stability of the resulting matrix factorization.

</details>


### [151] [Directional Kernel Mean Difference: A Fast Signed Statistic for Univariate Distribution Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20119)
*Shijie Zhong,Jiangfeng Fu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出方向核均值差（DKMD），一种保留分布偏移方向信息的单变量分布比较统计量，通过积分核均值嵌入差与奇函数权重实现，具有反对称性、对对称差异免疫和随机占优下的方向单调性。提出数据驱动的Riemann估计器保证渐近一致性，并开发了O(N log N)时间、O(N)内存的前缀-后缀扫描算法。实验表明DKMD能正确隔离方向偏移、对重尾异常值鲁棒，且可扩展到百万级样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统最大均值差异（MMD）通过平方RKHS距离丢弃了方向信息，无法区分分布偏移的方向（如左移还是右移）。本文旨在设计一种保留偏移方向的有符号统计量，同时保持核方法的理论优势。

Method: DKMD定义为核均值嵌入差与固定奇函数权重（如文献中常用的奇函数）的积分；提出基于数据驱动的Riemann和估计器保持渐近一致性；利用实数轴的全序性质设计O(N log N)前缀-后缀扫描算法实现高效计算。

Result: 实验表明DKMD能正确识别方向偏移（如均值左移或右移），对对称扰动（如方差变化）不敏感，对重尾异常值鲁棒（异常值可能导致均值差符号翻转），且能高效处理百万级样本。

Conclusion: DKMD作为一种有符号分布比较统计量，在保留方向信息的同时具有理论保证和计算效率，是对传统MMD的有力补充，特别适用于需要识别分布偏移方向的场景。

Abstract: We introduce the Directional Kernel Mean Difference (DKMD), a signed statistic for univariate distribution comparison that preserves the direction of distributional shifts. Unlike the squared Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD), which discards directional information by squaring the RKHS distance, DKMD integrates the difference of kernel mean embeddings against a fixed odd weighting function. This construction yields three structural properties: antisymmetry, immunity to symmetric distributional differences, and directional monotonicity under stochastic dominance. We derive a data-driven Riemann estimator that ensures asymptotic consistency with the continuous formulation, strictly preserving the theoretical guarantees of the signed statistic in empirical evaluations. To overcome the quadratic computational cost of kernel methods, we develop an $O(N \log N)$ prefix--suffix scanning algorithm that exploits the total order of the real line while requiring only $O(N)$ memory. Experiments on synthetic benchmarks demonstrate that DKMD correctly isolates directional shifts from symmetric perturbations, remains robust to heavy-tailed outliers that can flip the sign of the mean difference, and scales to millions of samples in seconds.

</details>


### [152] [Adaptive Bayesian Online Learning via Expert Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20239)
*Jungbin Jun,Ilsang Ohn*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种贝叶斯在线学习聚合框架，将不同的贝叶斯更新规则视为专家，根据序列预测损失进行聚合，并在在线共形推断和高斯过程回归中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯在线学习的性能高度依赖推理选择（学习率、先验分布、变分族等），这些选择通常在数据流到来之前就已固定，导致模型适应性不足。

Method: 将贝叶斯更新规则视为专家，根据序列预测损失聚合这些贝叶斯专家，通过理论证明该聚合能够与事后最优专家竞争，聚合成本由每个专家每轮表现的评价方式决定。

Result: 在在线共形推断中实现了带长期随机覆盖的平滑贝叶斯自适应共形推断；在高斯过程回归中实现了累积预测KL散度的oracle不等式，以及对未知Hölder光滑度的对数因子级自适应。

Conclusion: 该聚合方法能够在无需oracle专家选择的情况下追踪强专家，实验验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Bayesian online learning promises uncertainty-aware prediction on data streams, but its performance hinges on inferential choices, including learning rates, prior distributions and variational families, which are usually fixed before seeing the stream. We address this by treating Bayesian update rules as experts and aggregating the Bayesian experts according to sequential predictive losses. We prove that the resulting aggregate competes with the best expert in hindsight at an aggregation cost determined by how each expert's per-round performance is evaluated. We instantiate the framework in online conformal inference and Gaussian process regression. The conformal inference application yields a smoothed Bayesian counterpart of adaptive conformal inference with long-run randomized coverage, while the Gaussian process application gives an oracle inequality in cumulative predictive Kullback-Leibler risk and adaptation to unknown Hölder smoothness up to logarithmic factors. Experiments show that the aggregate tracks strong experts without oracle expert selection.

</details>


### [153] [Adaptive deep nonparametric regression from dependent data under covariate shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20309)
*William Kengne,Ehud Mossa Ockegna*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对协变量偏移和相依观测数据，提出了稀疏惩罚深度神经网络（SPDNN）估计器用于非参数分位数回归和Huber回归。在密度比未知时采用两步预训练策略，建立了非渐近误差界，并证明了该方法在Hölder光滑函数类中可自适应达到（对数因子内）极小化最优收敛率，适用于i.i.d.数据及多种经典时间序列模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际应用中源域和目标域数据分布不同（协变量偏移），导致标准度量不再适用。现有方法多假设i.i.d.数据，缺乏对相依观测和非参数回归场景下协变量偏移问题的统一理论分析。

Method: 1) 提出稀疏惩罚深度神经网络（SPDNN）估计器，通过密度比加权校正分布差异；2) 密度比未知时采用两步预训练：第一步用最小二乘SPDNN估计密度比，第二步用加权的SPDNN估计回归函数；3) 利用广义Bernstein型不等式处理i.i.d.、φ-混合、强混合和C-混合等多种依赖结构；4) 建立分位数回归和Huber回归的非渐近误差界。

Result: 在Hölder光滑函数类中，SPDNN估计器可自适应达到（对数因子内）极小化最优收敛率，该结果适用于i.i.d.数据以及φ-混合、强混合、C-混合等时间序列模型。

Conclusion: SPDNN估计器为协变量偏移下的非参数回归提供了统一且最优的理论框架，同时适用于独立和相依数据场景，具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Covariate shift often occurs because, in many real applications, the source and the target observations may be generated from different distributions. In this case, the standard metric under the source distribution is not appropriate. This paper considers deep neural network estimators for nonparametric quantile and Huber regression under covariate shift and from dependent observations. We deal with a generalized Bernstein-type inequality that is satisfied by many classical models, including i.i.d. observations, $φ$-mixing, strong mixing, and $\mathcal{C}$-mixing processes. To perform the covariate shift phenomenon, we propose a sparse-penalized deep neural network (SPDNN) estimator that takes into account the discrepancy between the source and target distributions of the data. When the density ratio (between the source and target distributions of the covariate) is unknown, a two steps pre-training procedure is carried out: the first step is devoted to the construction of a least squares SPDNN estimator of the density ratio; which is used in the second step to perform a pre-training reweighted SPDNN estimator of the regression function. For both the quantile and the Huber regression, non-asymptotic error bounds of the proposed SPDNN estimators are established in the class of Hölder smooth functions. These estimators can adaptively attain (up to a logarithmic factor) the minimax optimal convergence rate from i.i.d. data as well as from several classical time series models.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [154] [Estimating the Hüsler--Reiss variogram matrix by clipped moments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19672)
*Shuang Hu,Johan Segers*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文针对多元极值分析中弱尾部依赖导致估计偏差的问题，提出了基于低尾截断的Hüsler-Reiss多元广义Pareto模型的变差矩阵一阶和二阶矩估计量，证明了其渐近正态性，并通过模拟和实际数据验证了其在弱依赖场景下的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多元极值分析中，弱尾部依赖会导致基于极限多元广义Pareto分布的估计方法产生显著偏差，现有方法对此问题处理不足。

Method: 针对Hüsler-Reiss多元广义Pareto模型，构造基于随机向量低尾截断版本的变差矩阵一阶和二阶矩估计量，并建立其渐近正态性。

Result: 模拟研究表明，在分量间依赖较弱的情况下，所提估计量比经验变差估计量具有更低的偏差；实际数据（多瑙河流域洪水流量、美国航班延误）分析表明，基于一阶截断矩估计量的拟合模型尾部依赖结构与数据经验尾部依赖更吻合。

Conclusion: 提出的低尾截断矩估计量能有效缓解弱尾部依赖带来的估计偏差，在弱依赖场景下优于传统经验变差估计量。

Abstract: In multivariate extreme value analysis, the tail dependence between some of the risk variables at hand may be weak, even when other variables do tend to become large simultaneously. Weak tail dependence may induce a substantial bias in estimation procedures based on the limiting multivariate (generalized) Pareto distribution of excesses over high thresholds. We consider a Hüsler--Reiss multivariate generalized Pareto model and, motivated by this issue, propose first- and second-order moment estimators of its variogram matrix constructed from a lower-tail-clipped version of the underlying random vector. The asymptotic normality of the proposed estimators is established. We demonstrate by simulation studies that they have lower bias than the empirical variogram estimator in certain cases, particularly when the dependence between components is weak. The estimators are applied to flood discharge data from the Danube river basin and the US flight delay data, showing that the tail dependence structure implied by the fitted model based on the first-order clipped moment estimator aligns more closely with the empirical tail dependence of the data than that based on the empirical variogram estimator.

</details>


### [155] [Finite-horizon quantile martingale posteriors: raw-urn laws and matrix-gain regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19839)
*Nam Anh Le*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了鞅后验（Martingale posteriors）在分位数推断中的性质，特别是Pólya-urn后验的停止态分布、方差结构、多分位点联合分布，以及条件分位数回归中平滑鞅后验的Bernstein--von Mises定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鞅后验通过前向插补观测来量化不确定性，但实际实现中只能在有限次插补后停止。需要理论刻画这种停止后的统计性质，特别是分位数估计的方差和校准性。

Method: 采用Pólya-urn过程构造经验分位数的鞅后验，推导停止态的解析分布；引入随机逼近跟踪器（带冻结增益c）分析方差；通过密度自适应增益实现校准；对条件分位数回归，采用全逆Jacobian矩阵增益启动平滑鞅后验。

Result: 停止态分位数的方差保持熟悉的鞅尾和方差分数；冻结增益c的随机逼近跟踪器的方差包含显式因子G_a（a=cf_0(q_τ)），可能低于或超过尾分数；密度自适应增益通过无密度膨胀恢复校准；共享urn创新得到有限多个分位点水平的联合分布；全逆Jacobian矩阵增益满足过程Bernstein--von Mises定理，标量或对角增益则无法匹配sandwich协方差过程。

Conclusion: 鞅后验方法在分位数推断中具有严谨的理论保证，但增益选择至关重要：密度自适应增益可恢复校准性，全逆Jacobian矩阵增益是条件分位数回归中实现校准有限水平带的关键。

Abstract: Martingale posteriors quantify uncertainty by forward-imputing observations from one-step-ahead predictive distributions, but implementations stop after finitely many imputations. For the empirical Pólya-urn posterior of a quantile the law of the stopped state is derived. The quantile of the stopped urn measure keeps the familiar martingale tail-sum variance fraction; the deployed stochastic-approximation tracker with frozen gain $c$ does not. Its variance carries an explicit factor $G_a$ with $a=cf_0(q_τ)$, which may fall below or exceed the tail fraction, and a density-adapted gain restores calibration through a density-free inflation. Shared urn innovations yield the joint law of finitely many quantile levels. For conditional quantile regression, a smoothed martingale posterior started at the ordinary quantile-regression estimator with a full inverse-Jacobian matrix gain satisfies a process Bernstein--von Mises theorem with calibrated finite-horizon bands; scalar or diagonal gains cannot match the sandwich covariance process.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [156] [Native Multi-Dimensional Subquadratic Operators via Input Dependent Long Convolutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19378)
*David R. Wessels,Farhad Ramezanghorbani,David W. Romero,Alireza Moradzadeh,Olivia Viessmann,Maksim Zhdanov,John St. John,Ken Janik,David M Knigge,Yucheng Tang,Erik J Bekkers,Saee Gopal Paliwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HyenaND是一种亚二次、全局、输入相关的算子，可直接作用于多维数据的原生几何结构，通过隐式参数化的全局多维卷积核实现，其CUDA实现nSubQ融合FFT-卷积路径，在长上下文基因组学、计算机视觉、医学影像和PDE建模等任务中，纯HyenaND堆栈匹配强注意力基线，混合配置则超越纯注意力和基于递归的混合模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的亚二次注意力替代方案在处理多维数据时需要妥协：标准卷积缺乏全局感受野和输入依赖性，而递归模型需要将图像、体积和PDE等数据栅格化为违反其空间结构的临时1D扫描顺序。

Method: 提出HyenaND算子，通过隐式参数化的全局、输入相关多维卷积核直接作用于多维数据的原生几何结构；CUDA实现nSubQ融合FFT-卷积路径，将O(L log L)缩放转化为实际加速。

Result: 在长上下文基因组学、计算机视觉、医学影像和PDE建模中，纯HyenaND堆栈匹配强注意力基线性能；混合配置（HyenaND与注意力层交错）超越纯注意力和基于递归的混合模型。

Conclusion: HyenaND为多维数据提供了有效的亚二次注意力替代方案，在保持全局感受野和输入依赖性的同时，混合配置能进一步提升性能。

Abstract: Subquadratic alternatives to attention require compromises when applied to multi-dimensional data: standard convolutions lack global receptive fields and input dependency, while recurrent models require rasterizing data such as images, volumes, and partial differential equation (PDE) into an ad-hoc $1\rm D$ scan order that violates their spatial structure. We introduce \textit{HyenaND}, a subquadratic, global, input-dependent operator that acts directly on the native geometry of multidimensional data through convolutions with implicitly parametrized global, input-dependent multi-dimensional convolutional kernels. Our CUDA implementation, \texttt{nSubQ}, fuses the FFT-convolution path to turn HyenaND's $\mathcal{O}(L \log L)$ scaling into wall-clock speedups. Across long-context genomics, computer vision, medical imaging, and PDE modeling, pure HyenaND stacks match the accuracy of strong attention baselines, while hybrid configurations that interleave HyenaND and attention layers outperform both pure attention and strong recurrence-based hybrids.

</details>


### [157] [Bayesian Wind Tunnels for Model Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19379)
*Siddhartha R Dalal,Vishal Misra,Abhay Parekh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究Transformer能否进行贝叶斯模型选择（识别正确的假设类别）。通过引入“模型选择贝叶斯风洞”受控环境，发现2.8M参数的Transformer能在纯关系中实现与贝叶斯最优近乎一致的熵（0.01-bit差异），并扩展到非嵌套比较。关键发现是：当判别统计量需要算术运算时，模型在整数token上成功但在不透明符号上完全失败，且该边界在112倍参数扩展后依然存在。探针实验显示前沿LLM在同类任务上表现出定性贝叶斯行为但存在校准差距（~55x）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作证明Transformer能在固定假设类内进行精确贝叶斯滤波，但能否进行贝叶斯模型选择（即从数据中识别正确的假设类别）尚未可知。本文旨在探究Transformer执行模型选择的能力及其边界条件。

Method: 构建“模型选择贝叶斯风洞”受控环境，使用不动点对合（f(f(x))=x）与3-循环等关系，通过整数token和不透明符号两种表征方式，在不同参数规模（2.8M至316M）下训练Transformer，并与贝叶斯最优进行比较。同时设计头部子任务诊断和概率探针评估。

Result: 1) 2.8M参数Transformer在纯关系任务上达到0.01-bit熵一致性；2) 非嵌套比较（对合vs.3-循环）中类后验MAE低于0.001；3) 算术运算条件下，整数token成功但符号表征完全失败，且该边界在112倍扩展后仍存在；4) 固定重标记的符号表征成功（0.009-bit MAE）；5) 头部诊断定位失败原因为头部反转与算术的组合；6) 前沿LLM定性贝叶斯但校准差距~55x。

Conclusion: Transformer能够进行真正的贝叶斯模型选择，但存在关键限制：算术运算需要稳定的语义表征才能实现电路编译；模型不能泛化到算术场景中的不透明符号，这一限制随规模扩展依然刚性存在。

Abstract: Prior work has shown that transformers can perform exact Bayesian filtering within a fixed
  hypothesis class. Can they also perform Bayesian model selection -- identifying the correct
  hypothesis class from data? We introduce model-selection Bayesian wind tunnels: controlled
  environments where ground-truth posteriors over hypothesis classes are available in closed
  form. Using fixed-point-free involutions -- whose defining property f(f(x))=x is purely
  relational -- a 2.8M-parameter transformer achieves 0.01-bit entropy agreement with the
  Bayesian optimum (3 seeds), with both integer tokens and opaque symbols whose meanings
  change every episode. This extends to non-nested comparisons: involutions vs. 3-cycles
  (where neither class is a subset of the other) achieve class-posterior MAE under 0.001,
  demonstrating genuine model selection beyond simplicity/subset bias. We then identify a
  sharp perceptual access condition: when the discriminative statistic requires arithmetic --
  modular addition (rotations) or multiplication (f(x)=cx mod p) -- model selection succeeds
  with integer tokens but fails completely with opaque symbols, and this boundary persists
  under 112x scaling (2.8M to 316M parameters). A stationarity control confirms the operative
  factor: opaque tokens with a fixed relabeling succeed (0.009-bit MAE), showing that stable
  semantics, not integer identity, enable circuit compilation. Header subtask diagnostics
  localize the failure to the composition of header inversion with arithmetic rather than
  header parsing itself. Probing frontier LLMs on the same tasks shows qualitative Bayesian
  behavior but a large calibration gap (~55x), measured through lossy probes and therefore
  directional rather than exact.

</details>


### [158] [Total Variation Distance Estimation in Autoregressive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19510)
*Eric Price,Kevin Tian,Zhiyang Xun,Yusong Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在三种访问模型下，估计两个自回归语言模型之间总变差距离（TV距离）的样本复杂度问题，提出了更优的查询上界和下界，并在实际推理引擎（SGLang vs vLLM）上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM部署中即使使用相同的权重，不同的推理引擎（如批处理、自定义kernel、量化等优化）也会导致输出分布存在显著差异。准确衡量这种差异对于模型安全性和一致性验证至关重要，但现有TV距离估计方法效率较低。

Method: 论文在三种访问模型下设计了TV距离估计算法：(1) 样本访问（sample access），提出$\widetilde{O}(n^2 K/\varepsilon^2)$查询复杂度的估计器，改进了Meel等人的结果；(2) logit访问，给出$O(n/\varepsilon^2)$的紧界；(3) 带噪logit访问，实现样本与logit访问之间的平滑插值，复杂度为$\widetilde{O}((n+n^2σ^2)/\varepsilon^2)$。

Result: 理论上推导了三种访问模型下的查询复杂度上下界，其中logit访问达到了紧界；实验上测量了SGLang和vLLM两个推理引擎在相同权重下的TV距离，验证了算法在KL散度为无穷时仍可有效估计TV距离的鲁棒性和实用性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的TV距离估计算法在理论上提供了更优的查询复杂度保证，在实践中也证明了有效性，尤其适用于KL散度不可用或无穷大的场景，为LLM部署一致性评估提供了可靠工具。

Abstract: Modern LLM deployments use a number of implementation choices and inference optimizations (e.g., batching, custom kernels, and quantization) on top of fixed weights, so two engines serving "the same model" can produce meaningfully different distributions. We study the problem of estimating the total variation (TV) distance between two length-$n$ autoregressive distributions to additive error $\varepsilon$, under three access models. (1) Under sample access, we use $\widetilde{O}(n^2 K/\varepsilon^2)$ queries, where $K$ is the maximum support of the next-token distribution. This improves upon the $\widetilde{O}(n^3 m/\varepsilon^5)$-query estimator of Meel et al. (2025), where $m \geq K$ is the total size of the token alphabet. (2) Under logit access, we use $O(n/\varepsilon^2)$ queries, and this is tight. (3) Under noisy logit access, we smoothly interpolate between the above two guarantees: if probability values are given to relative error $σ$, we use $\widetilde{O}((n+n^2σ^2)/\varepsilon^2)$ queries. We complement our theoretical results with an empirical evaluation of our algorithms, for example measuring the distance between SGLang and vLLM serving identical weights. Our experiments highlight the robustness and practicality of estimating the total variation distance, which remains estimable where the KL divergence is infinite. Our code is available at https://github.com/XunZhiyang/llm-tv-estimation.

</details>


### [159] [Efficient Clustering with Provable Guardrails for LLM Inference at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19704)
*Longshaokan Wang,Wai Tsang Keung,Punit Ghodasara,Roman Wang,Ali Dashti,Francesc Moreno-Noguer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一个两阶段聚类算法（Mini-batch K-Means + 集合覆盖的Johnson-Chvatal启发式），在确保每个样本与簇代表之间的相似度下限和类别属性精确匹配的同时，实现了线性时间复杂度和十亿级可扩展性，在3800万用户的生产部署中将推理成本和延迟降低50倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模LLM应用的推理成本和延迟成为瓶颈，现有聚类方法无法在数十亿样本规模下同时保证：1) 每个样本与其代表的最小相似度；2) 类别属性的精确匹配；3) 可扩展性。

Method: 两阶段算法：第一阶段使用Mini-batch K-Means生成初始簇；第二阶段在每个初始簇内使用Johnson-Chvatal集合覆盖启发式贪心选择代表，在嵌入空间的α球内强制保证相似度和属性约束。时间复杂度O(nd + n²d/K)，内存复杂度O(nd + n²/K²)。

Result: 在内部和公开数据集上，该方法不仅提供每样本的约束保障，而且运行速度比其他常见聚类方法快10-1000倍，可扩展到大多数标准方法无法处理的数据规模。在3800万客户的生产部署中，将下游成本和延迟降低50倍，同时保持个性化效果。

Conclusion: 该聚类方法通过可证明的约束保证和线性复杂度，成功解决了大规模LLM应用的推理瓶颈问题，并已在生产环境中验证了其有效性，为基于LLM的高效推理提供了实用的数据缩减方案。

Abstract: Scaling LLM-based applications to millions of users is bottlenecked by the inference cost and latency of modern foundation models. A natural fix is to cluster the inputs and call the LLM only on cluster representatives, letting other members inherit the output -- but this is only safe if each member is measurably close to its representative. Existing clustering methods do not offer such per-sample quality control at scale: none jointly guarantee a minimal within-cluster similarity, exact matching of categorical attributes, and scalability to tens of millions of samples. We propose a two-stage algorithm that generates initial clusters with Mini-batch K-Means, then greedily selects representatives within each initial cluster -- a step equivalent to the Johnson-Chvatal heuristic for Set Cover over alpha-balls in embedding space. The algorithm enforces the similarity and attribute guardrails exactly by construction, and runs in $O(nd + n^2 d/K)$ time and $O(nd + n^2/K^2)$ memory for $n$ samples, feature dimension $d$, and $K$ initial clusters -- linear in $n$ when $K$ grows proportionally with $n$. We provide benchmarks against common clustering methods on internal and public datasets: our method not only delivers per-sample guardrails but also runs 10-1000x faster and scales to data sizes where most standard methods become intractable. Deployed on 38 million customers for a persona-based recommender, the clustering method cut downstream cost and latency by 50-fold while preserving personalization and unblocked the production launch.

</details>


### [160] [Asymptotically Optimal Regret for Reinforcement Learning without Horizon Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19854)
*Runlong Zhou,Zihan Zhang,Maryam Fazel,Simon S. Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对有限时域时间齐次表格型马尔可夫决策过程，提出了一种无遗憾界中H依赖的新算法，实现了渐近最优的Ō(√(SAK) + S⁸A³)遗憾上界，完全消除了log H因子，并通过截断地平线、切割奖励函数和新的总偏差界等技术克服了值函数时变性的技术困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消除有限时域MDP遗憾界中对时域H的对数依赖，实现与上下文赌博机下界匹配的渐近最优无H遗憾界。此前Zhang et al. (2021)的遗憾界包含log H因子，而Zhang et al. (2022)的界虽无H但S依赖过重（S⁹A³）。

Method: (1) 利用最优值函数V_h^*在h上的单调性；(2) 将值函数非平凡地投影到S维网格上，避免联合界带来的min{log H, S}因子；(3) 提出地平线截断参数，实现基于奖励的探索，消除单独的无奖励探索阶段；(4) 设计切割奖励函数(cutting bonus)，同时保持乐观性和规划所需的单调性；(5) 证明时间齐次MDP的新总偏差界，控制切割奖励函数中的裁剪方差项，实现多项式的S依赖且无H依赖。

Result: 遗憾上界为Ō(√(SAK) + S⁸A³)，其中K为回合数，Ō隐藏多项式对数因子。该界不含H，且渐近匹配Ω(√(SAK))下界。相比Zhang et al. (2021)的Ō(√(SAK log H) + S²A log H)完全消除了log H，相比Zhang et al. (2022)的Ō(√(S⁹A³K))大幅降低了S依赖。

Conclusion: 本文实现了有限时域时间齐次表格型MDP的渐近最优无H遗憾界，核心创新在于利用值函数单调性、投影技巧和三项关键技术工具（地平线截断、切割奖励函数、总偏差界），为消除时域依赖提供了系统性方案。

Abstract: We study horizon-free regret minimization for finite-horizon time-homogeneous tabular Markov decision processes with $S$ states, $A$ actions, horizon $H$, and per-trajectory total reward bounded by $1$.
  We propose a new algorithm and prove a regret upper bound \[\tilde O(\sqrt{SAK}+S^8A^3)\] with failure probability $δ$, where $K$ is the number of episodes and $\tilde O(\cdot)$ hides $\mathsf{poly}\log(S,A,K,1/δ)$.
  Thus, the regret is $H$-free and asymptotically optimal, matching the contextual-bandit lower bound $Ω(\sqrt{SAK})$ up to logarithmic factors. This completely removes the $\log H$ dependence from the previous $\tilde O(\sqrt{SAK\log H}+S^2A\log H)$ guarantee of Zhang et al. (2021), and drastically improves the prior best horizon-free regret $\tilde O(\sqrt{S^9A^3K})$ of Zhang et al. (2022) asymptotically.
  The main technical difficulty is that the optimal value functions $\{V_h^*\}_{h=1}^H$ are time-inhomogeneous even though the transition kernel is time-homogeneous. A direct union bound over all value functions typically incurs an additional $\min\{\log H,S\}$ factor. We avoid this factor by (i) exploiting the monotonicity of $V_h^*$ in $h$ and (ii) non-trivially projecting the value functions onto an $S$-dimensional grid.
  Our analysis relies on three additional ingredients. First, we introduce a horizon-truncation argument that enables reward-based exploration and removes the cost of a separate reward-free exploration phase. Second, we design a cutting bonus that preserves both optimism and the monotonicity needed for planning. Third, we prove a new bound on total deviation for time-homogeneous MDPs, which controls the clipped variance terms in the cutting bonus with adjustable polynomial dependence on $S$ and without any dependence on $H$. Together, these tools yield an asymptotically optimal horizon-free regret guarantee.

</details>


### [161] [Active Inference as a Convex Markov Decision Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20152)
*Nikola Milosevic,Nicolás Hinrichs,Nico Scherf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将主动推理（AIF）框架重构为策略优化问题，证明在闭环控制策略下，期望自由能（EFE）最小化可表述为凸马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）。其中，实用项等价于潜在MDP中的奖励最大化，而认知项引入非线性成分。文章分析了有限时域、折扣和平均奖励三种EFE形式，推导出镜像下降算法，并指出将世界模型学习与策略优化结合赋予了主动推理"表演性强化学习"的结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将主动推理（AIF）与强化学习（RL）之间的理论联系形式化，尤其是澄清期望自由能（EFE）最小化与标准奖励最大化之间的关系，并为AIF提供现代优化理论和收敛性分析基础。

Method: 将EFE最小化重构为凸马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），其中实用项（pragmatic terms）为线性项等价于潜在MDP中的奖励最大化，认知项（epistemic value）为非线性项。推导镜像下降（MD）算法对目标函数进行局部线性化，生成策略依赖奖励，兼容演员-评论家方法和动态规划。

Result: 证明了EFE最小化可表述为凸MDP；揭示了认知驱动力是策略依赖的表演性奖励；推导出有限时域、折扣和平均奖励三种EFE形式的镜像下降算法；指出AIF与表演性强化学习（performative RL）的结构等价性。

Conclusion: 主动推理可以通过表演性强化学习的框架与现代RL和优化理论对接，为收敛性分析和策略改进保证提供了理论路径。

Abstract: Active Inference (AIF) frames adaptive behavior as the minimization of expected free energy (EFE), combining epistemic and pragmatic objectives within a single variational principle. We frame AIF as policy optimization and show that, for closed-loop control policies, EFE minimization can be formulated as a convex Markov decision process (MDP). In this formulation, the pragmatic terms are linear in the predictive state marginals and therefore equivalent to reward maximization in a latent MDP, while the epistemic value introduces a nonlinear component that distinguishes EFE minimization from standard reinforcement learning. This perspective further reveals the epistemic drive of active inference as a policy-dependent (performative) reward. We analyze finite-horizon, discounted, and average-reward formulations of EFE and derive a mirror descent (MD) algorithm that locally linearizes the objective around the current state marginals, yielding a policy-dependent reward that is compatible with actor-critic methods and dynamic programming. Finally, we argue that coupling world-model learning with policy optimization gives active inference the structure of performative reinforcement learning, providing a route toward grounding active inference within modern reinforcement learning and optimization theory, including convergence analysis and principled policy improvement guarantees.

</details>


### [162] [CruiseBench: A Real-Flight-Aligned N-CMAPSS Benchmark for Engine RUL Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19380)
*Pu Cheng,Qiang Miao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 CruiseBench，一个基于 N-CMAPSS 数据集的巡航阶段剩余寿命（RUL）基准，通过引入 CPM-N-CMAPSS 掩码提取巡航时段，并采用固定预处理协议（排除虚拟传感器、保留原始分辨率、应用RUL上限），为RUL模型提供可复现的受控对比基准。实验表明TSMixer在该基准下表现最优，消融研究验证了飞行阶段选择、时间降尺度方法及RUL上限阈值对结果的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: N-CMAPSS数据集虽然通过真实飞行剖面和全航段时间序列增加了 realism，但全飞行记录导致数据量大、退化信号与工况变化纠缠，预处理选择复杂，直接比较RUL建模性能困难。作者旨在通过提取巡航阶段、制定固定协议来消除工况干扰，实现可控且可复现的RUL模型对比。

Method: 提出 CPM-N-CMAPSS（巡航时段掩码），采用共同高度法识别九个可访问子数据集的巡航区间；CruiseBench 对掩码行应用固定协议：仅使用场景描述符和测量传感器作为输入，排除虚拟传感器、健康参数和辅助元数据，保留原始分辨率窗口，并应用数据集级RUL上限。在LSTM、GRU、TCN和TSMixer上进行基线实验。

Result: 在 CruiseBench-eta5-W256-S10 设置下，TSMixer 取得最低平均RMSE（3.4±1.71）和 Saxena分数（(2.50±2.99)×10⁴）。消融研究表明飞行阶段选择、时间降尺度方法和RUL上限阈值显著影响报告结果。

Conclusion: CruiseBench 通过固定巡航阶段协议提供了可复现的RUL模型比较子基准；CPM-N-CMAPSS 为未来的迁移学习和领域自适应研究提供了阶段特定的数据基础。

Abstract: Remaining useful life (RUL) prediction estimates how long an engine can continue safe operation and is central to maintenance planning. N-CMAPSS extends C-MAPSS by simulating run-to-failure aero-engine trajectories using recorded real-flight profiles and retaining complete within-flight time series rather than cycle-level snapshots. However, this added realism reduces evaluation control because full-flight records increase data volume and entangle degradation cues with operating-regime variation, complicating preprocessing choices and direct comparisons of RUL modeling performance. To mitigate this issue, this paper proposes CruiseBench, a cruise-stage RUL benchmark derived from N-CMAPSS. It introduces CPM-N-CMAPSS (Cruising-Period Mask for N-CMAPSS), a mask artifact that stores cycle-local cruising intervals identified by the common-altitude method for the nine accessible subdatasets. CruiseBench applies a fixed protocol to the masked rows, using scenario descriptors and measured sensors as inputs while excluding virtual sensors, health parameters, and auxiliary metadata from the feature tensor, preserving native-resolution windows, and applying dataset-wise RUL caps. Experiments with LSTM, GRU, TCN, and TSMixer provide baseline results for this setting. Under CruiseBench-eta5-W256-S10, TSMixer obtains the lowest average RMSE, $3.4\pm1.71$, and Saxena score, $(2.50\pm2.99)\times 10^{4}$. Ablation studies show that flight-stage selection, temporal downscaling method, and RUL-cap threshold affect reported results. With its fixed cruise-stage protocol, CruiseBench provides a reproducible sub-benchmark for controlled RUL model comparison and CPM-N-CMAPSS provides a stage-specific data foundation for future transfer-learning and domain-adaptation studies.

</details>


### [163] [Air Quality Arena: A Large-Scale Multi-Region Ground Monitoring Dataset and Benchmark for Air Quality Forecasting with Time-Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19381)
*Rishi Bharadwaj,Manik Gupta,Pandarasamy Arjunan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了Air Quality Arena (AQA)，一个覆盖7个国家、4大洲、6种主要污染物、超过14,000个站点的多国多污染物空气质量数据集和基准，用于评估时间序列基础模型在短期空气质量预测中的表现。研究发现时间序列基础模型在零样本预测中表现优异，且结合视觉基础模型的跨模态架构效果最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空气污染每年导致约790万人过早死亡，准确预测空气污染水平是关键的公共卫生优先事项。然而，现有基准在地理范围和污染物覆盖上都很狭窄，且未能评估最新一代时间序列基础模型在大规模真实世界数据上的表现。

Method: 构建了AQA-Data（包含7个国家、4大洲、6种主要污染物、3年跨度、超过14,000个站点的多国多污染物数据集）和AQA-Bench（基准测试框架），在11个领先的时间序列基础模型和经典基线模型上评估短期空气质量预测性能。

Result: 时间序列基础模型是有效的零样本预测器，且持续优于经典基线方法；表现最佳的模型采用了跨模态架构，利用视觉基础模型进行时间序列预测。

Conclusion: AQA填补了现有空气质量预测基准在地理覆盖和污染物范围上的空白，验证了时间序列基础模型在空气质量预测中的有效性，特别是跨模态架构的潜力。数据集和基准已公开。

Abstract: Air pollution causes an estimated 7.9 million premature deaths annually, making accurate forecasting a critical public health priority. Machine learning is increasingly being applied to forecast air pollution levels, yet existing benchmarks remain narrow in both geographic scope and pollutant coverage, and fail to evaluate the latest generation of time series foundation models (TSFMs) on real world, large scale data. We present Air Quality Arena (AQA), a large scale multi-country and multi-pollutant dataset (AQA-Data) and benchmark (AQA-Bench) to address this gap. AQA covers 6 major pollutants over a three year period across 7 diverse countries and 4 continents, with more than 14,000 station-pollutant series, aiming to provide a comprehensive benchmark for air quality tasks. We benchmark this dataset across 11 leading time series foundation models and classical baselines to assess performance on short-term air quality forecasting. Our results demonstrate that TSFMs are effective zero-shot forecasters and consistently outperform classical baselines, with our top-performing model employing a cross-modal architecture that leverages a vision foundation model for time series forecasting. AQA is publicly released at AirQualityArena.github.io

</details>


### [164] [Challenges of Explainability in Continual Learning for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19382)
*Quentin Besnard,Emmanuel Doumard,Nicolas Labroche,Nicolas Ragot,Nicolas Ringuet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将可解释性作为理解持续学习中自适应时间序列预测的核心工具，在真实世界环境监测（地下水位）数据上，结合经验重放策略和注意力采样机制，通过注意力展开和Grad-CAM等方法分析预测行为与采样策略，揭示了归因模式随时间演化的规律及其对非平稳场景下数据选择和模型适应的指导意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在时间序列预测中表现出强大潜力，但在真实环境监测中面临非平稳动态和低可解释性的双重挑战，亟需理解持续学习框架下的模型行为。

Method: 采用PatchMixer、PatchTST、DLinear等神经预测架构，结合基于注意力的采样机制增强经验重放策略，利用注意力展开和Grad-CAM等归因方法分析预测行为与采样策略。

Result: 在具有异质模式和状态转移的真实地下水位时间序列上，可解释性分析揭示了归因模式随时间的演化规律，为数据选择和适应策略提供了有价值见解。

Conclusion: 可解释性不仅是分析工具，更是理解持续学习动态、指导非平稳场景下模型适应的关键手段，同时也揭示了当前方法面临的挑战与机遇。

Abstract: Deep learning models have shown strong potential for time series forecasting, yet their deployment in real-world environmental monitoring remains challenging due to non-stationary dynamics and limited explainability. In this work, we investigate explainability as a central tool for understanding continual learning in adaptive time series forecasting, with Experience Replay strategies. We study neural forecasting architectures such as PatchMixer, PatchTST and DLinear, augmented with attention-based sampling mechanisms to support model adaptation over time. Explainability is leveraged through attention rollout and gradient-based attribution methods (Grad-CAM) to analyze both predictive behavior and sampling strategies within a continual learning framework. Experiments conducted on real-world piezometric time series exhibiting heterogeneous patterns and regime shifts show that analyzing model and sampling behaviors provides valuable insights into the dynamics of the continual learning framework. Beyond predictive performance, our results highlight the challenges and opportunities of using explainability to understand continual learning behaviors, revealing how attribution patterns evolve over time and how they can inform data selection and adaptation strategies in non-stationary forecasting scenarios.

</details>


### [165] [SUM: Unified Geometric Surgery on Spatio-Temporal Adaptation Vectors for Federated Class Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19384)
*Jaeik Kim,Jaeyoung Do*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SUM框架，通过几何手术操作聚合服务器端的时空适应向量，统一解决联邦类增量学习中的空间干扰（异质客户端）和时间干扰（顺序任务）问题，无需额外客户端计算或通信开销，在多项视觉与语言基准上提升最高22%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦类增量学习（FCIL）中，联邦学习的客户端异质性（空间干扰）与持续学习的任务序列性（时间干扰）相互耦合，导致"时空灾难性遗忘"（ST-CF），现有方法用分离机制处理两类干扰，忽视了聚合过程中更新方向性交互的调控。

Method: 将FCIL重新解释为统一的多任务学习问题，客户端和任务更新均表示为共享参数空间中的适应向量；提出SUM框架，在服务器端对适应向量进行几何手术：空间SUM缓解轮内客户端级干扰，因果在线时间SUM消除跨任务干扰，不增加客户端计算/通信/内存开销。

Result: 在多种视觉和语言基准上，SUM相比现有FCIL方法提升最高22%，同时对不可靠客户端保持鲁棒性和计算效率。

Conclusion: 通过将FCIL统一为多任务学习视角，SUM以纯服务器端方式有效解耦并解决了空间和时间干扰，为联邦持续学习提供了高效且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Real-world intelligent systems often require both distributed collaboration across data-isolated clients and continual adaptation to evolving tasks. This setting naturally gives rise to Federated Class Incremental Learning (FCIL), which combines Federated Learning (FL) and Continual Learning (CL).
