This research investigates whether federated learning can support the development of robust models for predicting tumour progression in breast cancer patients while addressing four critical deployment pillars: transparency, scalability, security, and fairness. This study evaluates a federated learning framework using multimodal data, including clinical information, tumour characteristics, biomarker data, and patient demographics, alongside medical imaging data such as MRI scans, to model changes in tumour characteristics over time. The performance of the federated approach was compared with that of a centralised model trained on aggregated data. The report then further examines strategies to enhance secure model updates, maintain performance across patient subgroups, and support scalability across institutions. The findings assess whether federated learning can achieve predictive performance comparable to centralised learning while preserving data locality. These results contribute to understanding the feasibility of privacy-preserving, multimodal predictive modelling and support future applications such as digital twins to assist clinicians and patients in personalised treatment planning.

</details>


### [200] [Agent-Centric Animal Pose Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19548)
*Eyrun Eyjolfsdottir,Kristin Branson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种以智能体为中心的动物行为自回归生成模型框架，通过将感知和行动约束在生物体的自我参考系中，实现对个体和群体行为的建模。配套发布了通用库，支持多视角数据表示转换、行为分布拟合度量化评估，并在果蝇求偶行为中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解动物在算法层面的行为——包括注意什么、如何形成内部模型和计划、如何映射到动作——仍是神经科学和行为学的核心挑战。数据驱动的生成模型为理解动物行为提供了路径。

Method: 提出智能体中心的自回归生成模型框架：(1)输入为自我中心感官观察，输出为自我中心运动，模拟动物从自身参考系观察和行动；(2)社会行为通过各智能体独立感知和响应彼此而涌现；(3)框架需管理同一数据的多个并行表征（多视角转换）并进行ML特有的离散化等操作；(4)发布通用库，专注于这些表示之间转换的可组合操作序列。

Result: 训练的模型成功捕捉了果蝇求偶群体中社会行为的分布，库提供了量化拟合度测量工具，支持不同输入输出表征的系统比较，并能直接适应新领域。

Conclusion: 该智能体中心的自回归建模框架结合发布的通用库，为从追踪姿态数据中学习和分析动物行为提供了有效工具，能够捕获社会行为分布，支持跨表征的系统性比较，具有良好的泛化性。

Abstract: Understanding animal behavior at an algorithmic level -- what animals attend to, how they form internal models and plans, and how this maps to action -- remains a central challenge in neuroscience and ethology. Data-driven generative models offer a path toward this understanding. We introduce a framework for training agent-centric autoregressive models of animal behavior from tracked pose, applicable to single animals and to groups in which each agent senses and responds to its conspecifics. Our models input egocentric sensory observations and output egocentric movements, mirroring the biological constraint that animals observe and act on the world from their own reference frame. Social behavior emerges from agents independently sensing and responding to one another. This agent-centric formulation requires managing many parallel representations of the same data, along with ML-specific transformations like discretization. We release a general-purpose library focused on the composable sequences of operations that translate between these representations. We show that trained models capture the distribution of social behavior in groups of courting Drosophila, and our library includes quantitative tools for measuring fit. We demonstrate how the library supports systematic comparison across input and output representations and that it adapts straightforwardly to a new domain.

</details>


### [201] [The Mechanism Matters: When Knowledge Graphs Help Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19616)
*Mohammed Sameer Syed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过对照实验系统研究了知识图谱（KG）在强化学习（RL）中的作用，发现KG在组合稀疏奖励任务中能显著提升样本效率和求解可靠性，且增益来自图结构而非正则化；KG价值与任务相关知识量成正比；安全性取决于注入机制——软注入（如奖励塑形）能容忍错误知识，而硬掩码（action masking）在KG不完整时反而有害。临床案例表明，KG收益需要任务结构能被所选机制利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献以单领域、正面结果的方法论文为主，缺乏对KG在RL中何时有帮助、何时中性、何时有害的系统性理解。

Method: 通过合成可控KG在MiniGrid环境中独立变化RL任务、注入机制（状态特征、动作掩码、基于势能的奖励塑形）和KG质量，进行对照实验；并用UMLS衍生知识图谱在离线RL脓毒症管理案例中验证。

Result: 1) 组合稀疏奖励任务中，结构化KG指导将求解成功率从70%提升至97%，打乱KG边后优势消失（掩码p=0.0001，塑形p=0.006）；2) KG价值与任务相关知识含量成正比；3) 软注入容忍错误知识，硬掩码在KG不完整时有害；临床案例为谨慎的空结果。

Conclusion: 为从业者提供了关于使用KG指导RL时如何以及多大程度上信任KG的具体指导。

Abstract: Knowledge graphs (KGs) are widely used to inject prior knowledge into reinforcement learning (RL), yet the literature is dominated by single-domain, positive-result method papers, so we lack a systematic account of when KG structure helps an agent, when it is neutral, and when it hurts. We conduct a controlled study that independently varies the RL task, the injection mechanism (state features, action masking, or potential-based reward shaping), and KG quality. Using a synthetic, fully controllable KG over MiniGrid environments, we report three findings. First, on compositional sparse-reward tasks structured KG guidance improves sample efficiency and solve reliability (70% to 97% of seeds), and a shuffle control that permutes the KG's edges while preserving their count collapses the benefit toward baseline (masking p=0.0001; shaping p=0.006), so the gain is structural rather than generic regularization. Second, KG value scales with the amount of task-relevant knowledge the graph contains. Third, and most consequential, safety depends on the mechanism: soft, optimality-preserving injection benefits from correct knowledge and harmlessly ignores incorrect knowledge, whereas hard masking is brittle, forbidding essential actions when the KG is incomplete or corrupted and making a wrong KG worse than none. A UMLS-derived clinical case study on sepsis management under offline RL is a careful null, underscoring that benefits require task structure the chosen mechanism can exploit. Our results give practitioners concrete guidance on how, and how much, to trust a KG when using it to guide RL.

</details>


### [202] [SCPP: A Unified Python Library for Soft Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19620)
*Kiyan Rezaee,Morteza Ziabakhsh,Artin Bahrampour,Seyed Mohammad Ghoreishi,Asal Khaje,Ali Sajedifar,Manny Chalak,Ava Zerafatangiz,Sadegh Eskandari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCPP是一个开源的Python软聚类框架，提供了与scikit-learn兼容的统一接口，集成了40种代表性算法，并包含全面的基准测试、文档和自动化测试，支持可复现的实验和新算法扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的软聚类方法（模糊、概率、基于图、矩阵分解和深度学习）缺乏统一的、标准化的编程接口和评估框架，导致不同方法之间的比较和复现困难，阻碍了该领域的系统研究与应用。

Method: SCPP设计了一个规范化的、与scikit-learn兼容的估计器接口，统一了模型训练、预测、隶属度表示、评估和基准测试流程，集成了40种代表性软聚类算法，并提供了数据集、聚类质量指标、运行时/内存/可扩展性评估的全面基准测试套件。

Result: 成功构建了一个包含40种算法的统一框架，提供了标准化的评估基准（涵盖数据集、聚类质量指标和运行时/内存/可扩展性评估），并实现了与科学Python生态系统的无缝集成。

Conclusion: SCPP为软聚类研究提供了一个可复现、可扩展的开源工具，通过统一接口和标准化评估促进了不同软聚类方法的公平比较和系统研究。

Abstract: In this paper, we present SCPP (Soft Clustering Python Package), an open-source Python framework for soft clustering. SCPP establishes a canonical, scikit-learn-compatible estimator interface that standardizes model training, prediction, membership representation, evaluation, and benchmarking across heterogeneous soft clustering methods, including fuzzy, probabilistic, graph-based, matrix factorization, and deep learning methods. The framework currently integrates 40 representative algorithms together with a comprehensive benchmarking comprising datasets, clustering quality metrics, and standardized runtime, memory, and scalability evaluation. SCPP further provides extensive documentation, practical examples, automated testing, and seamless integration with the scientific Python ecosystem, enabling reproducible experimentation and straightforward extension with new algorithms. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/soft-clustering/soft-clustering.

</details>


### [203] [HypEMBER: Hypernetwork-based Ensemble for Robust Policy Learning of Parametrized Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19628)
*Nicolò Botteghi,Gabriele Pascali,Urban Fasel,Andrea Manzoni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HypEMBER框架，将超网络与集成学习结合，用于参数化动力系统的鲁棒强化学习控制，有效应对高维状态空间、模型不确定性和测量噪声，在Kuramoto-Sivashinsky方程和粒子导航任务中表现出优于现有方法的训练稳定性、样本效率和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RL方法在处理参数化动力系统时面临计算不可行性、鲁棒性差和泛化能力弱的问题，尤其在存在测量噪声、模型不确定性和参数变化的情况下，控制性能严重受限。

Method: 提出HypEMBER框架：(1) 使用超网络表示策略和价值函数，根据系统物理参数生成底层网络权重，实现跨不同动力学状态的参数化泛化；(2) 采用策略和价值近似器的集成学习来量化认知不确定性，改进探索策略并增强训练前后的鲁棒性。

Result: 在一维Kuramoto-Sivashinsky方程和二维时变涡流中粒子导航任务上验证，HypEMBER在训练稳定性、样本效率和对测量噪声及参数误指定的鲁棒性方面一致优于现有最先进的RL方法。

Conclusion: 超网络与集成学习的结合能有效提升RL框架在参数化动力系统控制中的鲁棒性和泛化能力，为实际工程中具有不确定性的复杂系统控制提供可行方案。

Abstract: In this work we investigate reinforcement learning (RL) as a framework for the robust control of parametrized dynamical systems in presence of measurements and model uncertainties. High-dimensional state spaces, expensive numerical solvers, the partial knowledge of the governing equations, and the dependence on physical parameters that may be uncertain or difficult to estimate accurately, make the use of standard RL approaches computationally unfeasible. Indeed, lack of robustness and poor generalization across parameter variations are further amplified in presence of noisy or incomplete measurements, ultimately hampering control performance. To address these challenges, we introduce HypEMBER, a novel RL framework based on the combination of hypernetworks and ensemble learning. In the proposed approach, both the policy and value functions are represented through hypernetworks that generate the weights of the underlying models conditioned on the physical parameters of the system, thereby enabling parametric generalization across different dynamical regimes. In addition, an ensemble of policy and value approximators is employed to quantify epistemic uncertainty, leading to improved exploration strategies and enhanced robustness during and after training. The performance of the proposed framework is assessed on two representative parametrized control problems: (i) the one-dimensional Kuramoto-Sivashinsky equation and (ii) a particle-navigation task in a two-dimensional time-dependent gyre flow, focusing on robustness with respect to measurement noise and parameter misspecification. Numerical results demonstrate that HypEMBER consistently improves training stability and sample efficiency, while achieving superior robustness to uncertainties affecting both the system dynamics and the available observations, in comparison with state-of-the-art RL methods.

</details>


### [204] [Anatomy of a Sound Neural Reasoner: One-Shot Amortization, First-Pass Poisoning, and Search Inertness in Clue-Rich Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19635)
*Aleksey Komissarov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文诊断了Lattice Deduction Transformer (LDT) 在数独推理中的行为：它本质上是一个单次预测器（one-shot predictor），而非学习型搜索过程。首轮前向传播即决定了几乎所有空格的填充（6x6全部，9x9达94-96%），失败发生在搜索开始前的"首轮投毒"（first-pass poisoning）。增强搜索组件（CoLT）仅减少重复无效推导，不改变可解实例集合。约束图注意力优于位置编码表，揭示优化效率而非容量差异。通过数字排列数据增强和测试时对称变换集成，可将硬切片准确率从72.8-78.9%提升至100%。在着色问题上单次行为消失。结论：在线索丰富场景下，准确率由校准性和对称性决定，搜索仅消除计算浪费。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经求解器被设计用于迭代推理，但LDT在数独任务中是否真正进行逐步搜索值得怀疑。作者旨在诊断LDT的实际行为，揭示其是单次预测器还是真正的搜索过程，并理解失败原因及改进方向。

Method: 通过控制实验系统诊断LDT：分析首轮前向传播的输出（首次投毒现象）；消融研究（CoLT各组件贡献）；对比约束图注意力与位置编码的性能差异；引入数字排列数据增强和测试时对称变换集成；迁移到图着色任务进行对比验证。

Result: LDT首轮前向传播即决定6x6所有空格和9x9 94-96%空格；增强搜索组件仅减少重复无效推导1,497倍，不改变可解性；约束图注意力在冻结训练预算下匹配完整模型，位置编码需更久训练；数字排列增强将9x9准确率从<1%提升至96.5±0.3%；对称变换集成将三个硬切片从72.8-78.9%提升至100%；图着色中无单次行为。

Conclusion: 在线索丰富的数独任务中，LDT类系统是单次预测器，准确率由校准性和对称性决定，搜索仅消除重复无效计算。有效的干预手段是训练数据增强和测试时对称性利用，而非改进搜索组件。

Abstract: Neural solvers are built to deduce, branch, and revise intermediate states. The Lattice Deduction Transformer (LDT) appears to do exactly that. In clue-rich Sudoku, it does not: one forward pass commits essentially the entire grid (every blank cell on standard 6x6, 94-96% on augmented 9x9), turning the iterative solver into a one-shot predictor wrapped in an exact verifier. All hard-slice failures are decided before search begins, when the first pass confidently deletes a value required by the true solution. We call this first-pass poisoning. Adding learned branching, MRV, backtracking, value exclusion, and shared nogoods (CoLT) does not change which Sudoku instances are solved; it cuts repeated invalid derivations 1,497-fold. At the frozen training budget, constraint-graph attention alone matches full-CoLT accuracy, while positional tables recover only under substantially longer training, indicating an optimization and sample-efficiency advantage rather than an absolute capacity difference. The diagnosis predicts two effective interventions. Digit-permutation augmentation raises 9x9 accuracy from below 1% to 96.5 +/- 0.3 across three training seeds on a symmetry-disjoint split. Test-time union over symmetry-transformed passes raises all three hard-slice checkpoints from 72.8-78.9% to 100% without retraining. On from-scratch graph coloring, one-shot behavior disappears and search changes accuracy. In clue-rich completion, LDT-like systems are one-shot amortized predictors rather than learned search procedures: accuracy is determined by calibration and symmetry, while search primarily removes computational waste.

</details>


### [205] [Expert-Guided Forecast Editing for Time-Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19659)
*Hung Le,Minh Hoang Nguyen,Manh Nguyen,Huu Hiep Nguyen,Dai Do*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DEFT提出了一个专家引导的预测编辑框架，通过将基础模型在趋势-季节分解空间中加以利用，并围绕其进行组件级细化的探索，在有限查询预算下平衡先验利用与结构化探索，从而显著提升时间序列基础模型的预测编辑效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列基础模型能够跨异构领域进行预测，但生成预测后无法直接融入特定任务的专家反馈。现有方法要么纯粹利用模型分布（Best-of-N），要么将预测视作无结构高维向量进行优化，两者各有局限，缺乏在有限查询预算下有效结合专家引导的策略。

Method: DEFT将预测分解为趋势和季节成分，先在分解空间中利用基础模型的原始样本，再围绕这些样本通过组件级细化进行探索。专家查询作用于完整轨迹，但评分被重用于分解后的趋势和季节成分，从而实现结构化组件级反馈。

Result: 在78个数据集、3种基础模型、4种反馈类型和7种查询预算下，DEFT相比Best-of-N、交叉熵方法和贝叶斯优化等基线方法持续提升专家引导的有效性。分子动力学案例进一步验证了该方法可推广至更具物理基础的反馈场景。

Conclusion: 稀疏的测试时引导应平衡先验利用与结构化探索，DEFT通过在分解空间中的组件级细化有效实现了这一原则。

Abstract: Time-series foundation models can forecast across heterogeneous domains without task-specific training, but their forecasts are fixed once produced and cannot directly incorporate task-specific expert feedback. We study expert-guided forecast editing: a frozen foundation model generates candidate future trajectories, and an expensive expert evaluator scores them to guide forecast revision. Under a tight query budget, two natural strategies sit at opposite ends: best-of-$N$ purely exploits the foundation model's predictive distribution, while optimization approaches mostly explore the forecast horizon as an unstructured high-dimensional vector. Each extreme is individually sub-optimal. We introduce \textbf{DEFT}, an expert-guided forecast editing framework that balances the two by first exploiting the foundation model's predictive samples in a decomposed trend--seasonal space, then exploring around them via component-wise refinement. DEFT queries the expert only on complete trajectories, then reuses scores for the trend and seasonal components that appeared in the queried recombinations. This lets each expert query provide structured component-level feedback while keeping the foundation model frozen. We compare DEFT against direct search approaches, including best-of-$N$, cross-entropy methods, and Bayesian optimization, under matched expert-query budgets. Across two forecasting benchmarks consisting of 78 datasets, three time-series foundation models, four feedback types, and seven query budgets, DEFT consistently improves the effectiveness of expert guidance. A molecular-dynamics case study further suggests that the same principle extends to more physically grounded feedback, supporting the hypothesis that sparse test-time guidance should be spent balancing prior exploitation with structured exploration.

</details>


### [206] [How Fast Can Reward Models Score? A Systems Study of C++ and PyTorch Inference Runtimes for RLHF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19712)
*Venkata Naga Sai Vishnu Rohit Pulipaka,Anish Katta,Deva Rohit Reddy Peddireddy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在RLHF流程中，奖励评分环节成为策略更新的瓶颈。作者构建了基于ONNX Runtime的C++推理引擎，在CPU上显著优于PyTorch eager模式、torch.compile和FastAPI；在GPU上虽优于PyTorch和FastAPI，但不如torch.compile。研究发现加速主要来自ONNX Runtime本身而非C++语言，且批处理策略的影响比语言和运行时的选择更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF流程中奖励评分（reward scoring）阻塞策略更新，而现有方案大多默认使用PyTorch eager模式或torch.compile，未检查是否真正高效。评分本身计算量小，但会与rollout生成争夺CPU和GPU资源，导致更快的评分引擎无法直接缩短步长时间，主要是释放资源供生成使用。

Method: 构建了基于ONNX Runtime的原生C++推理引擎。首先验证正确性（CPU误差5.7×10⁻⁶，GPU误差4.2×10⁻³）。然后在CPU和GPU上分别与PyTorch eager模式、torch.compile、FastAPI进行对比测试。进一步分析加速来源（ONNX Runtime vs C++语言本身）以及批处理策略的影响。

Result: CPU上：C++引擎全面超越所有基线，置信区间无重叠。GPU上：优于PyTorch和FastAPI，但不如torch.compile。加速主要来自ONNX Runtime而非C++。批处理策略对性能的影响比语言和运行时选择更大。

Conclusion: RLHF评分环节的优化应优先关注ONNX Runtime和批处理策略，而非单纯依赖PyTorch编译优化或语言选择。CPU场景下ONNX Runtime效果显著，GPU场景仍以torch.compile为优。

Abstract: In RLHF pipelines, reward scoring blocks policy updates. Slow scoring bottlenecks the entire loop, since no update runs until every rollout gets a score. And yet most setups just default to PyTorch eager mode or torch.compile, no one checks if that's actually fastest. Scoring itself is small. Rollout generation eats far more of a typical RLHF step. But scoring and generation fight over the same CPU and GPU resources, so a faster scoring engine doesn't shrink step time on its own. It mainly frees up capacity generation can use instead. We built a native C++ inference engine on ONNX Runtime. First step: confirm correctness. Output matched the PyTorch reference to 5.7 x 10^-6 on CPU and 4.2 x 10^-3 on GPU, close enough to trust. Then we tested it against PyTorch eager mode, torch.compile, and FastAPI, on both CPU and GPU. CPU was decisive. Our engine beat every baseline, confidence intervals didn't even overlap. GPU gave a different view: we beat PyTorch and FastAPI, but torch.compile came out ahead. Further testing traced the speedup to ONNX Runtime itself, not C++ as a language. And batching strategy mattered more than either the language or the runtime choice, more than we expected. The results are from repeated, independent runs, since single runs just aren't reliable enough to trust.

</details>


### [207] [Koopman Dreamer: Spectrally Constrained Latent Dynamics for Stable World-Model Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19719)
*Jiaqi Li,Xinglong Zhang,Haibin Xie,Yixing Lan,Wei Pan,Xin Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Koopman Dreamer，一种基于Koopman算子的Dreamer风格世界模型，通过谱约束的确定性潜动态核心（含二维旋转-缩放块）改进长期潜轨迹推演的稳定性，结合EMA教师目标与多步一致性训练，在连续控制任务中提升闭环性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜世界模型（如Dreamer）的神经转移在长程推演中缺乏对模态持久性和误差累积的直接控制，导致想象轨迹质量下降，影响样本效率和最终控制性能。

Method: 1) 用二维旋转-缩放块（谱约束）替代标准神经转移，刻画阻尼、旋转和近周期模式；2) 线性+低秩双线性动作项捕捉全局与状态相关控制效应，随机状态调制提供局部校正；3) 后验条件EMA教师目标+单步一致性、多步推演与开环观测预测损失；4) 推导多步推演误差上界，分离谱骨干与双线性交互的放大效应。

Result: 在DeepMind Control Suite和UAV-LiDAR自主导航任务上，Koopman Dreamer改善了长时域潜推演的稳定性，在依赖高质量多步想象的任务上取得了更强的闭环控制性能。

Conclusion: 谱约束的Koopman潜动态核心能有效平衡长程信息保留与误差衰减，为基于想象的世界模型提供更稳定的长期推演能力。

Abstract: Latent world models improve sample efficiency in continuous control by optimizing policies over imagined latent trajectories, but common neural transitions offer limited direct control over modal persistence and error accumulation in long rollouts. We propose Koopman Dreamer, a Dreamer-style world model with a spectrally constrained deterministic latent dynamics core. Its Koopman-inspired backbone uses two-dimensional rotation--scaling blocks with bounded radii to represent damping, rotation, and near-periodic modes. Linear and low-rank bilinear action terms capture global and state-dependent control effects, while stochastic-state modulation supplies local correction information. To reduce the mismatch between posterior-conditioned training and prior-only imagination, the model combines posterior-conditioned EMA teacher targets with one-step consistency, multi-step rollout, and open-loop observation-prediction objectives. We further derive a multi-step rollout-error bound that separates amplification by the spectral backbone and bilinear interaction from the additive effects of stochastic-state mismatch and modeling residuals, clarifying the trade-off between error attenuation and long-term information retention. Experimental results on proprioceptive continuous-control tasks from the DeepMind Control Suite and UAV-LiDAR autonomous navigation demonstrate that Koopman Dreamer improves the stability of long-horizon latent rollouts and achieves stronger closed-loop control performance on tasks that rely on high-quality multi-step imagination.

</details>


### [208] [Analytic Distribution of Classifier-Free Guidance for Schedule Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19725)
*Enze Jiang,Zheng Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了扩散模型中无分类器引导（CFG）的精确路径积分表示，揭示了它是通过指数路径积分修正来改变条件分布，而非简单的乘积分布。基于此分析提出了分布引导CFG（DG-CFG）调度策略，在Stable Diffusion 1.5上实现了更好的多样性-保真度权衡，并减少了达到目标图像质量所需的采样步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CFG的确定性引导动力学采样的分布无法用通常的乘积分布启发式$p_0^\omega q_0^{1-\omega}$准确描述，缺乏对CFG诱导分布的精确理论理解。

Method: 通过概率流ODE分析CFG，推导了恒定引导和时间依赖引导下诱导分布的精确解析路径积分表示；基于此提出DG-CFG调度策略，平衡时间步贡献并考虑信号强度和低噪声分数误差放大。

Result: 在带解析分数的玩具模型上验证了预测分布；在Stable Diffusion 1.5上，DG-CFG相比恒定和启发式调度提升了生成质量，实现了更强的多样性-保真度权衡，并在更少采样步数下达到固定图像质量目标。

Conclusion: DG-CFG在强引导导致饱和和质量下降的场景下尤其有效，能减少达到目标指标所需的采样代价。

Abstract: Classifier-free guidance (CFG) is the default mechanism for conditional generation in diffusion models, but the distribution sampled by its deterministic guided dynamics is not captured by the usual product-distribution heuristic $p_0^ωq_0^{1-ω}$. We analyze CFG through the probability flow ODE and derive exact analytic path-integral representations of the induced distributions for both constant and time-dependent guidance. The resulting formulas show that CFG modifies $p_{t_0}$ by an exponential path-integral correction, and that a time-dependent schedule enters this correction through the weight $ω(t)-1$. This characterization explains how score discrepancies accumulate along sampling trajectories and motivates Distribution-Guided CFG (DG-CFG), a schedule that balances timestep contributions while accounting for signal strength and low-noise score-error amplification. A toy model with analytic scores closely verifies the predicted distributions. On Stable Diffusion~1.5, DG-CFG improves generation and yields a stronger diversity--fidelity trade-off across guidance strengths, with especially clear gains when strong guidance causes saturation and quality degradation in constant and heuristic schedules. Across NFE budgets, DG-CFG reaches fixed image-quality targets with fewer sampling steps, reducing the sampling cost needed to achieve target metrics.

</details>


### [209] [The World Model Remembers, the Actor Forgets: Dream Rehearsal for Continual Model-Based RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19749)
*Gurp Nijjer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DreamV3系列模型在任务序列训练中会发生灾难性遗忘，即使使用无限回放缓冲区。研究发现遗忘根源是行为者（actor）崩溃，而非世界模型遗忘。通过冻结世界模型并使用有监督的自我模仿（基于世界模型自身的分级梦境），无需环境交互即可恢复丢失技能。该方法实现了无需任务标签、参数不变的持续学习，在4任务链（3/3种子成功）、8任务链（3/3种子成功）上均优于普通回放。梦境分级步骤是关键，论文识别了两种评分失败模式并提供了解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在持续强化学习中，模型常常会灾难性遗忘早先学习的任务。已有文献默认某一组件是遗忘的原因但从未实际测量过。本文旨在回答：究竟是哪个组件的遗忘导致了整体性能的崩溃？

Method: 1) 使用预注册的组件级探针（n=3种子），在无限回放缓冲条件下测量世界模型和actor各自对旧任务的保持情况。2) 通过干预实验：冻结世界模型，对比"在想象中强化学习"与"基于世界模型分级梦境的监督自我模仿"对丢失技能的恢复能力。3) 提出分级梦境排练（graded dream rehearsal）方法，在训练期间交错进行，实现无需任务标签的持续学习。4) 设计梦境评分规则，识别并解决两种评分失败模式，提供离线选择度量。

Result: 世界模型保持了对旧任务的几乎所有可测量信息（奖励区分保持比~1.0、价值估计、终止结构），而actor的行为崩溃。冻结世界模型后，在想象中强化学习无法恢复丢失技能（0/3种子），而基于分级梦境的监督自我模仿成功恢复（3/3种子，零环境交互）。分级梦境排练在4任务链（3/3种子成功vs普通回放0/3）、8任务链（3/3种子成功）上均有效，且优于匹配的真实经验克隆（配对差异+0.13，95% CI [0.07, 0.24]）

Conclusion: DreamV3在持续学习中的灾难性遗忘本质上是通道问题（channel problem）而非记忆问题（memory problem）——世界模型保留了足够的知识，但actor无法正确利用这些信息。通过将世界模型的梦境进行分级评分并用作监督信号进行自我模仿，可以有效解决持续学习中的灾难性遗忘，无需任务标签或增加参数。梦境评分步骤是该方法成功的关键。

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement-learning agents of the DreamerV3 family forget catastrophically when trained on task sequences, even when an unbounded replay buffer preserves every earlier experience. We ask a question the continual-RL literature has assumed an answer to but never measured: which component forgets? Under never-clear replay, pre-registered component-level probes (n=3 seeds throughout) show that the world model retains essentially everything measurable about old tasks -- reward discrimination (retention ratio ~1.0), value estimates, and termination structure -- while the actor's behavior collapses. Forgetting in this regime is a channel problem, not a memory problem. We demonstrate this by intervention: with the world model frozen and identical imagined rollouts, reinforcement learning in imagination fails to recover a lost skill (0/3 seeds), while supervised self-imitation on the world model's own graded dreams recovers it on 3/3 seeds with zero environment interaction. Interleaved during training, this graded dream rehearsal yields a task-label-free, parameter-constant continual learner: 3/3 four-task chains retained where plain replay passes 0/3, 3/3 eight-task chains, and consistent gains over matched real-episode cloning (paired difference +0.13, bootstrap 95% CI [0.07, 0.24], complete seed separation). The dream-grading step is load-bearing: we characterize two scoring failure modes, provide an offline selection gauge that caught both before they contaminated results, and give a realized-first grading rule that closes them. All experiments were pre-registered with committed protocols; every refuted hypothesis is reported.

</details>


### [210] [Convergence-Latency-Aware Adaptive Modulation and Resource Allocation in RIS-Assisted Wireless Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19759)
*Liwei Wang,Wen Chen,Jun Li,Qingqing Wu,Ming Ding,Xusheng Zhu,Qiong Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究RIS辅助的无线联邦学习系统，提出了一种联合考虑收敛性与延迟的自适应调制和子信道分配方案，通过推导符号错误率对FL损失衰减的影响，构建混合交替优化框架解决MINLP问题，实验证明该方法在复杂任务和挑战性无线场景下能实现更快收敛和更高测试精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线联邦学习在阻塞传播环境下因不可靠传输导致训练延迟大、收敛性差；现有研究缺乏对调制依赖的传输误差下学习收敛与通信延迟之间权衡的刻画。

Method: 通过表征符号错误对上传本地梯度的影响，推导出揭示符号错误率对FL损失衰减影响的收敛相关上界；基于此构建联合收敛-延迟优化问题（MINLP），采用低复杂度混合交替优化框架求解。

Result: 在MNIST、CIFAR-10和Speech Commands数据集上的实验表明，所提方案在收敛速度和测试精度上均优于现有自适应通信方案，尤其在复杂任务和挑战性无线场景下。

Conclusion: RIS辅助的自适应调制和子信道分配能有效提升无线FL在阻塞环境下的性能，所提优化框架在收敛-延迟权衡中取得显著优势。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) over wireless networks suffers from significant training latency and degraded convergence due to unreliable wireless transmission, especially under blocked propagation environments. Although reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (RISs) can improve communication reliability, existing wireless FL studies rarely characterize the trade-off between learning convergence and communication delay under modulation-dependent transmission errors. In this paper, we consider a wireless FL system operating under RIS-assisted blocked-link propagation scenarios, and focus on adaptive modulation and sub-channel allocation for convergence-latency aware communication design. By characterizing the effect of symbol errors on uploaded local gradients, we derive a convergence-related upper bound that reveals the impact of symbol error rate (SER) on FL loss decay. Based on this result, we formulate a joint convergence-latency optimization problem, which is cast as a mixed-integer nonlinear programming (MINLP) problem, and solve it using a low-complexity hybrid alternating optimization framework. Extensive experiments on MNIST, CIFAR-10, and Speech Commands show that the proposed scheme consistently achieves faster convergence and higher test accuracy than existing adaptive communication schemes, especially in complex tasks and challenging wireless scenarios.

</details>


### [211] [AlphaRoute: Large Language Models as Semantic Optimizers for Multi-Objective Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19768)
*Kabir Murjani,Mishri Bhavsar,Manish I. Patel,Jonti Talukdar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AlphaRoute是一个多目标自适应搜索框架，将VLSI全局布线中的拆线重布(R&R)转化为动态优化系统，利用SHAP分解、3D Dijkstra迷宫路由、自适应PathFinder策略，并通过大语言模型(LLM)作为语义策略优化器动态调整惩罚参数。在ISPD 2025基准测试中，MEMPOOL上拥塞降低98.6%，ARIANE设计上溢出减少29.8倍，罚分从SOTA的1.780降至0.0538。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统VLSI全局布线启发式算法依赖静态惩罚调度，无法有效处理复杂拥塞拓扑结构，而全局布线本身是NP-hard的3D网格分配组合优化问题，需要新的动态优化方法。

Method: 1) 基于SHAP的拥塞分解，隔离单条线网拥塞贡献；2) 针对性子图提取与3D Dijkstra迷宫路由；3) 自适应PathFinder策略；4) 利用LLM作为语义策略优化器，在确定性知识图谱约束下动态调整惩罚参数。

Result: 在ISPD 2025基准上，MEMPOOL拥塞降低98.6%；ARIANE设计溢出降至146,109（比SOTA减少29.8倍），罚分S_orig=0.0538 vs SOTA的1.780。

Conclusion: 优秀的算法搜索几何结构可以克服Python解释型实现带来的延迟开销，LLM作为语义优化器在VLSI布线中具有显著潜力。

Abstract: Very Large Scale Integration (VLSI) global routing is an NP-hard combinatorial optimization problem requiring signal net assignment across capacity-constrained 3D grids while minimizing congestion, wirelength, and via transitions. Because traditional heuristics rely on static penalty schedules that fail on complex congestion topologies, we present AlphaRoute: a multi-objective adaptive search framework reformulating rip-up and reroute (R&R) into a dynamic optimization system. We introduce SHAP-based overflow decomposition to isolate per-net congestion, driving targeted subgraph extraction via 3D Dijkstra maze routing and an adaptive PathFinder policy. Crucially, AlphaRoute employs Large Language Models (LLMs) as semantic policy optimizers. Bounded by a deterministic knowledge graph, the LLMs interpret congestion metrics to dynamically adjust penalty parameters. Evaluated on ISPD 2025 benchmarks, AlphaRoute reduces overflow by 98.6% on MEMPOOL. On the constrained ARIANE design, we achieve an overflow of 146,109 (a 29.8x reduction in overflow over the state of the art), yielding a penalized score of S_orig = 0.0538 versus the State-of-the-art (SOTA) 1.780. These results demonstrate that superior algorithmic search geometry can overcome the latency of interpreted Python implementations.

</details>


### [212] [An Isotropy-Preserving Spectral Cap for Muon: Theory and Three Case Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19771)
*Jiachun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一分析框架，基于损失在权重缩放下的精确尺度不变性假设（在归一化密集的网络中近似成立），解释了Muon矩阵符号优化器相比SGD会加速权重矩阵范数漂移（t^{1/2} vs t^{1/4}）的机制。作者提出一种轻量级"谱上限"方法，通过去除更新中顶部奇异方向的一阶增长来控制输出协方差，该操作与奇异值谱的最小熵相关。在nanoGPT前馈投影、64专家MoE路由器和bf16 FlashAttention的查询/键投影三个系统的实验中，谱上限提高了各向同性，防止了路由器坍缩到单一专家和注意力头接近发散等具体故障，同时验证损失基本不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon及相关矩阵符号优化器越来越多用于大语言模型预训练，但它们对单个权重矩阵内部几何结构的影响尚不清楚。本文旨在通过建立统一框架来理解这种影响，并探索控制潜在问题的轻量级方法。

Method: 1) 在精确尺度不变性假设下，理论分析SGD和Muon对权重矩阵范数演化的不同影响；2) 观察到谱范数扰动具有非负二阶项，提出"谱上限"方法——从每个更新中投影去除顶部奇异方向的一阶增长；3) 在三个小型系统上实验验证：nanoGPT前馈投影、64专家MoE路由器、bf16 FlashAttention的查询/键投影。

Result: Muon的矩阵符号步移除了SGD固有的1/||W||制动机制，导致Frobenius范数和谱范数以更快速率（t^{1/2} vs t^{1/4}）向外漂移。谱上限提高了各向同性，在路由器坍缩到单一专家和注意力头接近发散等边缘场景中防止了具体故障，同时验证损失基本不变。

Conclusion: 谱上限是一种轻量级方法，可以在不冻结训练的情况下控制输出协方差——权重通过非顶部方向、顶部方向旋转和顶部切换继续学习。但作者强调尺度不变性假设较强，目前结果只是小规模的初步研究，欢迎反馈。

Abstract: Muon and related matrix-sign optimizers are increasingly used to pre-train large language models, but their effect on the internal geometry of individual weight matrices is not well understood. This preliminary report proposes a unified framework built on a single idealizing assumption -- exact scale invariance of the loss under weight rescaling, which holds approximately in normalization-heavy networks. Under this assumption, plain SGD carries a built-in 1/||W|| brake on its update size, whereas Muon's matrix-sign step removes that brake, so both the Frobenius and spectral norms drift outward faster (t^{1/2} versus t^{1/4}). We further observe that the spectral-norm perturbation has a non-negative second-order term. This implies that a lightweight "spectral cap" -- which projects out only the first-order growth of the single top singular direction from each update -- can control the output covariance W K_X W^T without freezing training: the weight keeps learning through non-top directions, top-direction rotation, and top switching. We relate this cap to the min-entropy (H-infinity) of the singular-value spectrum. We then study three systems trained with Muon: a nanoGPT feed-forward projection, a 64-expert mixture-of-experts router, and the query/key projections of a bf16 FlashAttention block. In each case the cap increases isotropy and, at the margins -- a router collapsing to a single expert, and the near-divergence of one attention head -- prevents a concrete failure, while leaving validation loss essentially unchanged. We emphasize that the scale-invariance assumption is strong and that these small-scale results are preliminary; comments are welcome.

</details>


### [213] [OPIUM: Mitigating Steering Externalities and Over-Refusal via Dual Objective Latent Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19806)
*Kavin Aravindan,Arihant Rastogi,Aadi Prasad,Krishak Aneja,Saiyam Jain,Vaishnavi Shivkumar,Ponnurangam Kumaraguru*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OPIUM是一种无需训练的激活空间净化方法，通过表示匹配优化转向向量，在保持预期干预效果的同时，消除安全性与实用性之间的副作用，改善安全-效用权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活转向（Activation Steering）在推理时控制大语言模型时，转向向量会产生意外的外部性：效用向量可能削弱安全行为，而拒绝向量可能在良性提示上引发过度拒绝。需要一种方法在不牺牲性能的前提下消除这些副作用。

Method: OPIUM通过表示匹配（representation matching）优化新的转向向量：给定两组提示上的参考行为，在保留期望干预的下游表示的同时，使向量在原始向量失效的提示上匹配更安全的参考行为。该方法无需训练。

Result: 在转向外部性和过度拒绝两种设置下，OPIUM相比原始转向和方向消融（directional ablation）方法，显著改善了安全性与效用之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 有害的激活转向副作用通常可以直接在激活空间中得到缓解，OPIUM提供了一种轻量级、无需训练的解决方案。

Abstract: Activation steering provides a lightweight mechanism for controlling large language models at inference time, but steering vectors can have unintended externalities: utility vectors may weaken safety behavior, while refusal vectors may induce over-refusal on benign prompts. We introduce OPIUM (Optimizing Protected Injections via Utility Manifolds), a training-free method for sanitizing steering vectors through representation matching. Given reference behaviors on two prompt sets, OPIUM optimizes a new steering vector that preserves the downstream representations induced by the desired intervention while matching a safer reference behavior on prompts where the original vector fails. Across steering-externality and over-refusal settings, OPIUM improves the safety--utility tradeoff relative to vanilla steering and directional ablation, suggesting that harmful side effects of activation steering can often be mitigated directly in activation space.

</details>


### [214] [Auto-Fill: Learning to Predict Missing Values Accurately with Specialist Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19847)
*Yurong Liu,Yeye He,Haoyu Dong,Junjie Xing,Shi Han,Dongmei Zhang,Surajit Chaudhuri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Auto-Fill方法，通过后训练三个分别专精于世界知识、文本推理和代码推理的小语言模型（SLM），结合校准集成机制（动态选择最置信专家或弃权），以低于前沿模型1%的成本，在表格缺失值预测任务上达到超越o3-pro、Gemini 3 Pro、DeepSeek R1等大推理模型的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大推理模型在表格缺失值预测中虽潜力巨大，但部署成本高昂且易过度自信产生幻觉/误报。作者观察到该任务需要世界知识、文本推理和代码推理三种能力的独特组合，但现有单一模型难以高效兼顾。

Method: 针对三种核心能力分别后训练三个小型语言模型（SLM），每个专精一种能力；设计校准集成机制，动态选择最置信的专家模型进行预测，或在不确信时主动弃权，从而确保高精度。

Result: 在11个基准数据集、2200张真实表格上的实验表明，Auto-Fill在准确率上超越o3-pro、Gemini 3 Pro、DeepSeek R1等前沿模型，且推理成本仅为其不到1%。

Conclusion: 专业化分工与校准弃权策略在表格数据这一重要场景中效果显著，验证了用多个小型专用模型替代单一通用大模型在特定任务上的成本效益优势。

Abstract: Predicting missing cell values in tabular data is a fundamental problem in data cleaning. While state-of-the-art reasoning models show great promise in predicting missing values in tables, by reasoning holistically across rows and columns, they are costly to deploy at scale and tend to be overconfident, often generating hallucinated or false-positive predictions.
  In this paper, we observe that achieving high-precision missing-value prediction in tables requires a distinct combination of three capabilities: (1) world knowledge, (2) text-based reasoning, and (3) code-based reasoning. We systematically explore design choices for combining these capabilities, and propose an Auto-Fill approach that post-trains three specialist small language models (SLMs), each optimized for one capability. We develop a calibrated ensemble mechanism that either dynamically selects the most confident specialist or abstains, ensuring high accuracy.
  Extensive experiments on 11 benchmarks with 2200 real tables drawn from diverse domains show that Auto-Fill achieves superior accuracy compared to state-of-the-art reasoning models (e.g., o3-pro, Gemini 3 Pro, and DeepSeek R1), while operating at a fraction (less than 1%) of the cost of these frontier models. Our results highlight the effectiveness of specialization and calibrated abstention in the important domain of tabular data. Auto-Fill is publicly available at https://github.com/lyrain2001/auto-fill.

</details>


### [215] [Local Causal Structure Learning in the Presence of Latent Variables and Selection Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19866)
*Zheng Li,Hao Zhang,Ruxin Wang,Ruichu Cai,Kun Zhang,Feng Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LoCaLS算法，在允许存在潜变量和选择偏差的情况下，无需恢复全局因果结构即可识别目标变量的直接因果邻居，并通过理论保证和实验验证了其高精度与低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果发现方法要么学习全局结构导致计算成本高昂，要么假设不存在潜变量和选择偏差，这些假设在真实场景中常被违反。因此，本文旨在潜变量和选择偏差存在的情况下实现高效的局部因果结构学习。

Method: 首先刻画一个局部区域，在该区域上进行目标特定的因果发现而无需恢复全局结构；然后建立该局部区域上观测分布诱导的因果信息与全局因果结构中对应信息之间的理论桥梁；在此基础上提出LoCaLS算法，在标准假设下完备且正确地识别目标变量的直接原因和效应。

Result: 在随机和真实结构上的大量实验表明，LoCaLS相比现有局部方法持续获得更高的结构精度，同时比最先进的全局方法大幅降低计算开销。在两组真实基因表达数据集上的应用展示了生物学上合理的靶向因果结构。

Conclusion: LoCaLS在允许潜变量和选择偏差的条件下，以较低计算成本实现了与全局方法可比的局部因果结构识别能力，在大规模生物数据分析中具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Discovering the direct causes and effects of a target variable from observational data is a fundamental problem in causal discovery, with broad applications in domains such as gene regulatory analysis and biomedical research. Existing causal discovery methods either learn a global causal structure, which incurs substantial computational cost, or assume the absence of latent variables and selection bias, assumptions that are often violated in real-world settings. Motivated by these challenges, we study local causal structure learning in the presence of latent variables and selection bias. Specifically, we first characterize a local region that enables target-specific causal discovery without recovering the entire global structure. We then establish a theoretical bridge between causal information learned from the observed distribution induced on this local region and the corresponding information in the global causal structure. Building on these foundations, we propose LoCaLS, a local causal structure learning algorithm that is sound and complete under standard assumptions and identifies the same direct causes and effects of a target variable as those identifiable by global causal discovery methods, while allowing for latent variables and selection bias. Extensive experiments on random and real-world structures demonstrate that the proposed method consistently achieves higher structural accuracy than existing local methods while requiring substantially less computational effort than state-of-the-art global methods. Furthermore, applications to two real-world gene expression datasets reveal biologically plausible target-specific causal structures, demonstrating its practical applicability in large-scale biological data analysis.

</details>


### [216] [Nonlinear Bias-Compensated Adaptive Filter and Its Application for Time-Series Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19902)
*Yi Peng,Haiquan Zhao,Jinhui Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对现有非线性自适应滤波算法未充分考虑输入噪声、以及偏置补偿核最小均方(BCKLMS)算法存在字典固定导致信号表征不充分和鲁棒性差的问题，本文提出了随机傅里叶偏置补偿广义自适应滤波(RFFBCGA)算法，通过随机傅里叶特征、偏置补偿项和广义自适应函数，实现了固定网络结构、有效抑制输入噪声和提升多噪声场景鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非线性自适应滤波算法大多只考虑输出噪声而忽略输入噪声；BCKLMS算法虽能处理输入噪声，但存在两个主要局限：一是固定尺寸字典限制了网络增长，无法充分捕捉输入信号特征；二是基于LMS的算法在输出信号存在非高斯噪声时鲁棒性差。

Method: 提出随机傅里叶偏置补偿广义自适应滤波(RFFBCGA)算法：1) 采用随机傅里叶特征(RFF)构建偏置补偿(RFFBC)框架，保持固定网络结构同时通过BC项有效抑制输入噪声干扰；2) 利用广义自适应(GA)函数的灵活形式，增强算法在不同噪声场景下的鲁棒性。

Result: 通过大量仿真实验（包括真实世界时间序列预测任务）验证了所提方法的优越性。

Conclusion: RFFBCGA算法解决了BCKLMS的两个局限，在保持固定网络结构的同时更好地表征输入信号，并在多种噪声环境下展现出更强的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Most existing nonlinear adaptive filtering algorithms only account for output noise, neglecting the fact that input noise is also prevalent in practice. Although the recently proposed bias-compensated kernel least mean square (BCKLMS) algorithm addresses input noise in the nonlinear errors-in-variables (EIV) model, it still suffers from two major limitations. First, the use of a fixed-size dictionary restricts network growth but also prevents it from fully capturing the characteristics of the input signal. Second, as an least mean square (LMS) based algorithm, it exhibits poor robustness in the presence of non-Gaussian noise in the output signal. To overcome these issues, this paper proposes the random Fourier bias-compensated filter under general adaptive function (RFFBCGA) algorithm. Within the random Fourier feature based bias-compensated (RFFBC) framework, the proposed algorithm not only maintains a fixed network structure and effectively mitigates input noise interference through the BC term, but also achieves improved characterization of the input signal. Moreover, by leveraging the flexible form of the general adaptive (GA) function, the algorithm's robustness across various noise scenarios is further enhanced. Extensive simulations, including real-world time series prediction tasks, demonstrate the superiority of the proposed method.

</details>


### [217] [Time Series Network Utilization KPI Forecasting Using Advanced AI/ML Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19974)
*Niraj Gadhe,Kirti Bhardwaj,Moulik Jain,Shubhi Sharma,Vinay Saini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了多种时间序列预测模型（包括季节性分解、Prophet、随机森林、XGBoost、支持向量回归以及双向和卷积LSTM等深度学习架构）在网络带宽利用率预测任务上的性能，使用MAPE、NRMSE和R-square指标在通用接口数据集上进行比较，旨在为网络容量规划提供模型准确性与计算效率之间的权衡指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据密集型应用、云基础设施和物联网生态系统的快速增长使得主动式资源供给对维持网络性能至关重要，但传统的被动式方法无法准确预测流量波动，导致过度配置、意外停机和QoS下降，影响运营预算和业务连续性。

Method: 在通用接口数据集上，评估了季节性分解、Prophet、随机森林、XGBoost、支持向量回归以及双向LSTM和卷积LSTM等深度学习模型，并使用MAPE、NRMSE和R-square指标进行基准测试。

Result: 提供了不同模型在预测准确性与计算效率之间的权衡分析结果。

Conclusion: 本研究为工程师、运营商和企业主提供了可操作的见解，帮助他们根据具体基础设施需求选择最优的带宽利用率预测模型。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of data-intensive applications, cloud infrastructure, and IoT ecosystems has made proactive resource provisioning critical for maintaining optimal network performance. However, network administrators face a constant battle against capacity constraints, where traditional reactive approaches fail to accurately anticipate traffic fluctuations. This inability to foresee demand leads to costly over-provisioning, unexpected downtime, and degraded quality of service directly impacting operational budgets and business continuity. To achieve efficient capacity planning, accurate forecasting of bandwidth utilization is essential. This study addresses the challenge by evaluating a diverse spectrum of models including seasonal decomposition, Prophet, Random Forest, XGBoost, Support Vector Regression, and advanced deep learning architectures like bidirectional and Convolutional LSTMs - using a common interface dataset benchmarked across MAPE, NRMSE, and R-square metrics. Ultimately, this research delivers actionable insights into the trade-offs between model accuracy and computational efficiency, empowering engineers, operators, and business owners to select the optimal forecasting model for their specific infrastructure needs.

</details>


### [218] [Good Practice Guide for quantifying uncertainties for machine learning models applied to photoplethysmography signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19999)
*P. Harris,C. Bench,M. Rinkevičius,V. Marozas,L. Coquelin,A. Thompson,M. Nandi,U. Hackstein,P. J. Aston*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是QUMPHY项目的实践指南，系统综述了用于光电容积脉搏波（PPG）信号的机器学习模型及其不确定性量化方法，涵盖模型选择、回归/分类任务、模型依赖/无关的不确定性量化技术、验证方法、六个基准问题、相关软件及伦理考量，并给出总结与建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为可穿戴设备PPG信号的机器学习建模提供不确定性量化的系统性实践指导，填补该领域缺乏统一技术指南的空白。

Method: 采用指南综述形式，系统梳理了用于PPG信号的各类机器学习模型（回归与分类任务）、模型依赖与模型无关的不确定性量化方法、验证策略，并设计了六个基准问题及对应的基准数据集，同时配套提供了辅助实现软件。

Result: 形成了涵盖模型选择、不确定性量化实现与验证、基准问题与数据集、软件工具及伦理考量在内的完整实践指南框架。

Conclusion: 该指南为可穿戴PPG信号的机器学习与不确定性量化提供了高层次实践建议与标准化参考，有助于提升相关模型的可靠性与可解释性。

Abstract: This Good Practice Guide presents work done in the QUMPHY project (Uncertainty quantification for machine learning models applied to photoplethysmography signals) that considered both machine learning and uncertainty quantification for problems which used photoplethysmography (PPG) signals from wearable devices as input. It provides high-level guidance on what types of machine learning model might be used and how different models compare when applied to both regression and classification tasks. It provides guidance on the implementation of different methods for uncertainty quantification, covering both model-dependent and model-independent techniques, and on the validation of the results provided by those methods. It also describes six benchmark problems together with pointers to different benchmark datasets for each problem. Software is described that can assist practitioners in implementing the methods described herein and there is a brief consideration of ethical issues. It concludes with a summary and recommendations.

</details>


### [219] [Post-Training in Time Series Foundation Models: A Unifying Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20002)
*Shifeng Xie,Ambroise Odonnat,Zehao Xiao,Lei Zan,Malik Tiomoko,Lujia Pan,Themis Palpanas,Boris N. Oreshkin,Chenghao Liu,Keli Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了时间序列基础模型（TSFM）的后训练方法，将其划分为参数适配、上下文增强、模型组合、输出处理与不确定性控制、压缩与特化五大类别，并讨论了各方法的局限性及未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练的TSFM在下游部署时面临领域偏移、任务异质性、监督有限和计算约束等问题，需要后训练方法进行干预以弥合预训练与可靠部署之间的差距。

Method: 基于预测流程中的干预位点，将TSFM后训练方法分为五类：参数适配、上下文增强、模型组合、输出处理与不确定性控制、压缩与特化，并对每类中的代表性方法进行分析和讨论。

Result: 建立了TSFM后训练方法的统一分类框架，识别了当前各类方法的局限性，并指出了五个未来研究方向：受控适配、可靠上下文构建、不确定性感知模型组合、校准输出处理以及部署感知特化。

Conclusion: 通过提供统一的TSFM后训练方法框架，旨在帮助未来研究在预训练TSFM与下游可靠部署之间探索设计空间。

Abstract: Time series foundation models (TSFMs) have emerged as general-purpose models for time series analysis, but pretraining alone is often insufficient for reliable downstream deployment. Bridging this gap requires further intervention to handle domain shift, task heterogeneity, limited supervision, and computational constraints, which motivates post-training as a broad class of methods to adapt, augment, compose, calibrate, or specialize pretrained TSFMs for downstream tasks. In this work, we analyze TSFM post-training methods based on their locus of intervention in the prediction pipeline, yielding five categories: parameter adaptation, context augmentation, model composition, output processing and uncertainty control, and compression and specialization. Within each category, we study main representative methods and discuss their current limitations. We further identify future directions toward controlled adaptation, reliable context construction, uncertainty-aware model composition, calibrated output processing, and deployment-aware specialization. Overall, by providing a unifying framework for the emerging TSFM post-training landscape, this work aims to support future research to navigate the design space between a pretrained TSFM and its reliable downstream deployment.

</details>


### [220] [Generalized Kalman filter based temporal difference reinforcement learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20010)
*Vasos Arnaoutis,Eric Lutters,Bojana Rosić*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于条件期望理论的广义时序差分强化学习框架，将值函数和Q函数视为不确定量，通过随机推断问题求解，并利用多项式混沌展开或集成近似实现计算可行性，在线性与非线性最优控制问题上验证了其准确估计值函数及其不确定性的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典卡尔曼时序差分学习依赖线性-高斯假设，无法推广到非线性模型和非高斯分布，因此需要一种更通用的框架来处理值函数估计中的不确定性。

Method: 基于条件期望理论构建广义TD学习框架，将值函数估计视为随机推断问题；递归估计条件期望及其二阶概率矩以量化不确定性；采用多项式混沌展开或集成近似对随机问题进行离散化，实现计算可行。

Result: 在质量-弹簧-阻尼线性系统和封闭腔非线性热传导问题上，该方法能够准确估计值函数及其不确定性，并将经典卡尔曼TD学习推广到了更广泛的随机系统类别。

Conclusion: 该框架成功突破了线性-高斯假设的限制，将基于卡尔曼滤波的TD学习扩展到非线性、非高斯场景，为不确定性感知的强化学习提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a generalized temporal-difference (TD) reinforcement learning framework based on the theory of conditional expectations. The value and action-value (Q-value) functions are treated as uncertain quantities, and their estimation is formulated as a stochastic inference problem. Unlike classical Kalman-based temporal-difference learning, which relies on linear-Gaussian assumptions, the proposed formulation is derived directly from the conditional expectation framework and naturally extends to nonlinear models and non-Gaussian probability distributions. The proposed method recursively estimates not only the conditional expectation of the value function but also its second probabilistic moment, thereby quantifying the uncertainty associated with the learned value function throughout the learning process. To obtain a computationally tractable algorithm, the stochastic problem is discretized using either polynomial chaos expansions or ensemble-based approximations, providing efficient representations of the underlying random variables. The proposed framework is demonstrated on two optimal control problems: a linear mass--spring--damper system and a nonlinear heat conduction problem in a closed cavity. The numerical examples illustrate the capability of the proposed method to accurately estimate both the value function and its associated uncertainty, while extending classical Kalman-based temporal-difference learning to a broader class of stochastic systems.

</details>


### [221] [Zero-Shot Heart Rate Variability Forecasting from Consumer Wearables Using Time Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20027)
*Luukas Peräkylä,Fahad Sohrab,Ville Hautamäki,Merja Heinäniemi,Sui Huang,Pekka Abrahamsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了三种时间序列基础模型（TimesFM、Chronos、MOIRAI）在真实世界可穿戴设备HRV数据上的短时预测性能，并引入了一种保留变异性的插补方法处理数据碎片化问题。结果表明TSFMs无需微调即优于传统基线，Chronos和TimesFM表现最佳，MASE在0.81-0.87之间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短时心率变异性（HRV）预测可为临床提供自主神经功能障碍和不良心脏事件的预警时间。然而，消费级可穿戴设备产生的HRV信号存在碎片化和伪影问题，传统预测方法难以有效处理这些数据。

Method: 在49名健康个体的真实可穿戴数据上，评估三种时间序列基础模型（TimesFM、Chronos、MOIRAI）与传统基准（均值法、指数平滑法、加权移动平均法）的HRV预测能力。针对数据碎片化问题，提出一种保留变异性的插补方法，在经典线性插值基础上引入局部自适应随机噪声。

Result: TSFMs无需微调即全面超越传统基线，在32和64步两种上下文长度下平均MASE在0.81-0.87之间。Chronos和TimesFM为表现最佳模型，MOIRAI相对于基线提升有限。预测视界最长可达2小时。

Conclusion: TSFMs在真实可穿戴HRV数据上展现出优于传统方法的预测能力，为临床部署建立了基线。领域特定的微调是未来提升临床适用性的有前景方向。

Abstract: Short-term Heart Rate Variability (HRV) forecasting could provide clinicians with actionable lead time for detecting autonomic dysfunction and adverse cardiac events. Consumer wearable devices generate fragmented, artifact-rich HRV signals that challenge conventional forecasting approaches. In this study, we evaluated the forecasting ability of three Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs), TimesFM, Chronos, and MOIRAI, against traditional baselines (Mean, Exponential Smoothing, and Exponentially Weighted Moving Average) on real-world wearable data collected from 49 healthy individuals. To address data fragmentation, we introduce a variability-preserving imputation method that augments linear interpolation with locally adaptive stochastic noise, retaining physiological dynamics essential for accurate forecasting. The results show that TSFMs outperformed all baselines without fine-tuning, achieving average Mean Absolute Scaled Error (MASE) between 0.81 and 0.87 across TSFMs and both context lengths (32 and 64 time steps), with Chronos and TimesFM as the top models, though MOIRAI showed limited gains over baselines. With up to a 2-hour forecast horizon, the results establish a baseline for TSFMs' performance on a real-world dataset, highlighting domain-specific fine-tuning as a promising direction for clinical deployment.

</details>


### [222] [Test Case Prioritization for DNNs via Neural Collapse Instability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20046)
*Chunyu Liu,Mingyuan Li,Yang Li,Wenmin Li,Fei Gao,Tengfei Tu,Su-Juan Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NCIP框架，利用神经坍缩（Neural Collapse）特性，通过跨检查点预测变异性替代传统置信度信号，在有限测试预算下提升深度神经网络的早期故障发现能力，在多个数据集和架构上取得了1.5-20.6%的RAUC增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试用例优先级排序技术依赖单检查点的输出概率置信度信号，但DNN可能自信地错误预测，且预测类与竞争类之间的置信度边际通常很小，导致早期故障发现效果不佳。

Method: NCIP框架包含两个关键组件：(1) 利用分类器权重的等角性分数（成对余弦相似度的标准差）选择NC引导的代表性训练检查点子集；(2) 根据测试输入在所选检查点上的预测变异性进行优先级排序，暴露边界附近且易发生故障的样本。

Result: 在多个数据集和架构上的实验表明，NCIP相比竞争基线，在相同测试预算下RAUC-ALL提升1.5-16.6%，RAUC-500提升4.9-20.6%，并在所有数据集-模型对上取得最佳平均性能。

Conclusion: NCIP通过利用终端训练阶段的神经坍缩特性，用跨检查点预测变异性替代绝对置信度，有效提升了DNN在有限预算下的测试优先级排序和早期故障发现能力。

Abstract: With the widespread deployment of deep neural networks (DNNs) in safety-critical domains, reducing the cost of model validation under limited testing budgets has become increasingly important. Existing test case prioritization techniques often rely on single-checkpoint confidence signals derived from output probabilities. However, DNNs can be confidently wrong, and the confidence margin between the predicted and competing classes is frequently small, which weakens early fault discovery. To address this limitation, we propose a Neural-Collapse-Inspired Prioritization (NCIP) framework that replaces absolute confidence with cross-checkpoint prediction variability in the terminal training regime, where model geometry becomes highly structured. NCIP introduces two key components. First, it selects an NC-guided representative subset of training checkpoints using an equiangularity score of classifier weights, quantified as the standard deviation of pairwise cosine similarities among class weight vectors. Second, it prioritizes test inputs by their prediction variability across the selected checkpoints, surfacing boundary-adjacent and failure-prone samples that are unstable under checkpoint-induced decision boundary shifts. Extensive experiments across multiple datasets and architectures show that NCIP achieves strong performance in early fault discovery compared with competitive baselines, with 1.5 to 16.6 percent RAUC-ALL gains and 4.9 to 20.6 percent RAUC-500 gains under the same testing budget. NCIP further attains the best average performance across all dataset-model pairs.

</details>


### [223] [Evaluating and Mitigating Gender Bias in Pre-trained Embeddings for ML-based Recruitment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20073)
*Farnaz Faramarzi Lighvan,Lynn Houthuys*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了9种预训练嵌入模型在合成FairCVdb数据集上的表现，分析了性别泄露问题，并采用多任务对抗学习框架和帕累托前沿模型选择来平衡预测效用与公平性。实验表明，显式性别擦除能减少但无法消除性别泄露，对抗学习主要改善原始简历的公平性，是文本级去偏的补充而非替代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于AI的招聘系统使用在历史简历数据上训练的机器学习模型，可能延续和放大社会偏见。非结构化简历文本中，预训练语言模型嵌入即使在显式性别指标被移除后，仍可能推断出性别等敏感属性，这一问题亟待解决。

Method: 在合成FairCVdb数据集上评估9种预训练嵌入模型；采用多任务对抗学习框架（梯度反转）在预测应聘者适合度的同时抑制性别信息；使用基于帕累托前沿的多目标模型选择来平衡预测效用与公平性。

Result: 显式性别擦除能显著减少但无法完全消除性别泄露；对抗学习主要改善原始简历（未擦除性别）的公平性；对抗学习是文本级去偏的补充策略而非替代方案。

Conclusion: 对抗学习与文本级去偏应结合使用，单一策略不足以彻底消除AI招聘系统中的性别偏差。

Abstract: AI-based recruitment systems that rely on machine learning models trained on historical CV data, risk perpetuating and amplifying social biases. A key challenge arises in unstructured CV text, where pre-trained language model embeddings may infer sensitive attributes such as gender even after explicit indicators are removed. In this paper, we evaluate nine pre-trained embedding models on the synthetic FairCVdb dataset, analyzing the informativeness of their embeddings for applicant scoring and their susceptibility to gender leakage, on both original and gender-scrubbed biographies. We further use a multi-task adversarial learning framework with gradient reversal to predict applicant suitability while suppressing gender information from learned representations. Finally, we use a multi-objective Pareto-front-based model selection to balance predictive utility and fairness. Our experimental results show that explicit gender scrubbing substantially reduces but does not eliminate gender leakage, while adversarial learning improves fairness mainly on original biographies and acts as a complementary strategy rather than a substitute for text-level debiasing.

</details>


### [224] [Co-Evolving LLM Evaluators and Policies via DynamicRubric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20083)
*Beining Wang,Weihang Su,Hongtao Tian,Hao Kong,Tao Yang,Ting Yao,Qingyi Pan,Yueyue Wu,Qingyao Ai,Min Zhang,Yiqun Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DynamicRubric框架，通过响应集条件化的评估器-策略协同进化机制，为每个候选集生成加权二元评价标准并聚合为响应级分数，有效解决了策略优化中因响应质量接近导致的评估分数差距塌缩问题。实验表明，DynamicRubric在8B骨干网络上优于70B奖励模型和235B静态评估器，并在推理和编码任务上取得提升，已在微信搜索AI回答场景中全量上线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当策略改进导致采样响应质量接近时，评估器分数差距塌缩会产生弱化或误导性的策略监督信号，形成策略优化的瓶颈。现有方法缺乏对这种相对分数差距重要性的理论分析和针对性解决方案。

Method: 通过概率分配视角理论证明：在策略梯度更新中，两个响应之间的概率质量转移方向性增益恰好等于它们之间的评估器分数差距。基于此提出DynamicRubric框架，为每个候选集动态生成加权二元评价标准，并聚合为响应级分数，实现评估器与策略的协同进化。

Result: 在8B骨干网络上，DynamicRubric在评估器表现和策略监督强度上均优于70B奖励模型和235B静态评估器基线。优化后的策略在可验证推理和编码任务上也有提升。该系统已在微信搜索AI回答场景全量部署，服务每日千万级请求，关键线上指标改善。

Conclusion: 评估器引导的后训练应遵循一个原则：评估器应当与它们所监督的策略共同进化。

Abstract: Post-training with evaluator feedback on policy-induced samples serves as a major mechanism for improving large language models. As policies improve, these sampled responses become close in quality. These close candidates create a bottleneck for policy optimization: collapsed relative evaluator score gaps yield weak or misleading policy supervision. We theoretically characterize why these gaps matter through a probability allocation view, showing that the directional gain of shifting probability mass from one response to another is exactly the evaluator score gap between them. This identifies relative score gaps as the policy optimization signals that guide updates. Motivated by this view, we propose DynamicRubric, a response-set-conditioned evaluator--policy co-evolution framework that generates weighted binary rubric items for each candidate set and aggregates the resulting judgments into response-level scores. In our experiments with 8B backbones, DynamicRubric improves evaluator performance and provides stronger policy supervision than baselines using a 70B reward model or a 235B static rubric generator. DynamicRubric-optimized policies also show gains on verifiable reasoning and coding tasks. A DynamicRubric-optimized model is fully deployed in WeChat Search's AI answering scenario, where it serves all online traffic across tens of millions of requests per day and improves key online metrics. These results suggest a principle for evaluator-guided post-training: evaluators should evolve with the policies they supervise.

</details>


### [225] [Autonomous Collaborative Learning Among an Ensemble of Tsetlin Machines with Consensus-Based Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20124)
*Yehuda Rudin,Osnat Keren,Michal Yemini,Alexander Fish*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向纵向特征划分场景的图灵机去中心化协作学习范式，通过共识推理整合多智能体局部模型，无需原始数据交换，在网格与连通图拓扑下实现与集中式模型相当的分类精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图灵机联邦学习研究较少，去中心化与分布式TM学习领域缺乏探索；实际场景中多智能体拥有异构数据采集手段、局部数据分布与计算资源，亟需一种无需中心服务器、不交换原始数据的去中心化协作学习范式。

Method: 提出去中心化协作学习范式，采用纵向特征划分设置；每个智能体维护私有TM模型，不交换原始数据；通过共识推理将各智能体模型预测结果融合为全局决策；支持异构TM智能体，适用于多模态感知等场景。

Result: 在二维网格与连通图网络拓扑下的实验表明，去中心化模型的分类准确率与集中式模型相当。

Conclusion: 该去中心化范式可在不共享原始数据、支持异构智能体的条件下实现与集中式模型可比的分类性能，为分布式TM学习提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Tsetlin Machine (TM) is a rule-based machine-learning algorithm comprising collectives of two-action Tsetlin Automata (TAs) that cooperatively form conjunctive logical clauses from Boolean inputs through stochastic feedback. Although few recent studies have examined TM Federated Learning, the broader area of distributed and decentralized TM learning has not received much attention in the existing literature and warrants further exploration. In this work, we propose a paradigm for decentralized collaborative learning under a vertical feature-partitioning setting among an ensemble of Tsetlin Machines using consensus-based inference. Within this decentralized paradigm, each agent maintains its own private TM model, and there is no exchange of raw data among agents. Inference combines individual agents model predictions into a global consensus. The paradigm accommodates heterogeneous TM-based agents with differing data acquisition means, local data distributions, or computational resources, thereby facilitating the integration and fusion of information in settings such as multi-modal sensing environments. Experiments conducted using two-dimensional grid and connected graph network topologies demonstrate that the classification accuracies achieved are comparable to those of centralized models.

</details>


### [226] [CURED: Creating, Understanding, and Repairing Errors Demonstrator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20140)
*Nicholas Chandler,Sebastian Jäger,Philipp Jung,Felix Bießmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个结合机器学习与数据库管理系统的统一演示平台，允许用户上传表格数据、注入真实的数据依赖错误，并利用现代ML方法进行错误检测与清洗，旨在弥合理论进展与实践洞察之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据中的错误检测与清洗是数据密集型应用的前提，现有研究虽展示了统计学习算法的潜力，但缺乏一个直观的、集成化的工具来桥接理论算法与实际应用之间的鸿沟。

Method: 开发了一个基于Web的演示应用，集成作者近期关于ML驱动数据清洗与错误模型的研究成果，支持用户上传表格数据、注入数据依赖错误，并应用现代机器学习方法进行错误检测和清洗。

Result: 提供了一个可访问的在线演示平台（https://cured.demo.calgo-lab.de/），实现了从理论到实践的直观数据错误分析与清洗流程。

Conclusion: 该演示工具有效弥合了错误模型与数据清洗算法方面的理论进展与直观实践洞察之间的差距，为表格数据错误处理提供了可交互的实用平台。

Abstract: Detecting and cleaning errors in tabular data is a prerequisite for data intense software applications. Recent research at the intersection of Machine Learning (ML) and Database Management Systems (DBMS) highlights the potential of statistical learning algorithms for error detection and cleaning. This paper combines our recent work on ML-based data cleaning and error models in a unified demonstrator. The web application allows users to upload tabular data, perturb the data with realistic data dependent errors and use modern ML methods to clean and understand error mechanisms in data. Our demonstrator helps to bridge the gap between theoretical advancements and intuitive practical insights in the context of error models and data cleaning algorithms for tabular data. The demonstrator is available at https://cured.demo.calgo-lab.de/

</details>


### [227] [Local Stability and Gaussian Smoothing of Quantized Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20153)
*Sergey Salishev,Anton Makarov,Oleg Granichin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究了高斯平均化作为量化神经模型的平滑替代方法，推导了局部维度相关的|f-g|边界，计算了ReLU和符号激活函数的高斯平均闭式解，并在高维二元感知器上验证了该方法在推理端平滑和训练端平滑替代梯度中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化神经模型存在不连续性问题，导致梯度估计困难。作者希望通过高斯平滑技术为离散/量化神经网络提供可微分的替代方案，从而稳定训练过程并改善推理性能。

Method: 1) 提出高斯平均化作为量化神经模型的平滑替代；2) 在有界局部振荡条件下推导局部维度相关的|f-g|边界；3) 计算ReLU和符号激活函数的高斯平均闭式解；4) 在高维二元感知器上，通过显式量化噪声替代进行层预激活聚合，得到用于推理平滑和训练平滑替代梯度的高斯包络。

Result: 推导了有界局部振荡条件下的维度相关误差界，得到了ReLU和符号激活函数的高斯平均解析形式，并在高维二元感知器上验证了高斯包络在推理平滑和训练梯度替代中的有效性。

Conclusion: 高斯平均化是一种有效的量化神经模型平滑技术，能够在不连续网络稳定性分析中发挥作用，并为量化网络的训练提供可微分的梯度替代。

Abstract: We study Gaussian averaging as a smooth surrogate for quantized neural models. Under bounded local oscillation, we derive a local dimension-dependent bound on |f-g|, linking Gaussian smoothing to the stability analysis of discontinuous networks. We compute closed-form Gaussian averages of the rectified linear unit (ReLU) and sign activation functions, and illustrate the mechanism on a high-dimensional binary perceptron, where layer-preactivation aggregation under an explicit quantization-noise surrogate yields the Gaussian envelope used in inference-side smoothing and training-side smooth surrogate gradients.

</details>


### [228] [Self-organizing Architecture of Receptron Units: a Hardware-Aware Framework for Edge Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20162)
*Stefano Radice,Ludovico Casaccia,Riccaro Emanuele Beccalli,Bruno Paroli,Paolo Milani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Receptron模型的类神经形态分类器，该单单元架构无需多层网络即可实现非线性决策边界，专为资源受限的中端微控制器设计并支持连续在线自适应，在基础数据集上达到与标准机器学习方法相当的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网边缘设备（微控制器单元）面临严重的计算和内存限制，传统深度学习方法无法直接部署，亟需一种轻量级、可解释且支持在线学习的分类方案。

Method: 基于Receptron模型设计类神经形态分类器，采用单单元架构（非多层网络）实现非线性可分决策边界，支持在中端MCU上直接部署并进行连续在线自适应。

Result: 在基础数据集基准测试上进行交叉验证，精度与标准机器学习方法基线兼容（达到相当水平）。

Conclusion: Receptron可作为资源受限的类神经形态边缘系统在动态非平稳环境中的可行且可解释的替代方案。

Abstract: The growing demand for intelligent processing at the edge of IoT networks is constrained by the severe computational and memory limitations of microcontroller units, which render impractical conventional deep learning approaches. We propose a neuromorphicinspired classifier based on the Receptron model, a single-unit architecture capable of implementing non-linearly separable decision boundaries, without resorting to multi-layer networks. The model is designed for direct deployment on mid-range MCUs, while supporting continuous on-device adaptation. Experimental evaluation on basic dataset benchmarks yields cross-validated accuracies compatible with standard machine learning method baselines. These results position the Receptron as a viable and interpretable alternative for resource-constrained neuromorphic edge systems operating in dynamic, non-stationary environments.

</details>


### [229] [Instance Hardness-Based Relevance for Imbalanced Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20173)
*Vitor M. Leitao,Juscimara G. Avelino,George D. C. Cavalcanti,Rafael M. O. Cruz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于实例难度的相关函数（InHaR），用于识别回归问题中的罕见实例。与传统仅依赖目标分布的相关函数不同，InHaR引入了学习难度，能更好处理双峰分布等复杂场景，在重采样策略中显著提升预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统回归中的罕见实例识别依赖相关函数，但其基于目标数值分配固定相关值，在双峰分布等复杂场景下无法有效区分真正罕见实例与正常实例，导致不平衡学习方法的有效性受限。

Method: 提出Instance Hardness-based relevance function (InHaR)，将学习难度（实例难度）纳入相关函数，使罕见性推断不仅依据目标分布，还考虑实例对学习算法的难易程度。

Result: InHaR能正确识别双峰分布下的罕见区域，并引导Random Oversampling和Gaussian Noise等重采样策略，相比传统相关函数方法显著提升预测性能。

Conclusion: InHaR通过引入学习难度改进罕见实例识别，在不平衡回归任务中优于传统基于目标值的相关函数方法，尤其适用于双峰分布等复杂场景。

Abstract: Imbalanced regression problems arise when the target variable has an asymmetric distribution, resulting in underrepresented value ranges in the dataset. Traditional approaches for identifying rare instances rely on a relevance function that assigns higher importance to specific regions of the target distribution. However, the effectiveness of imbalance-aware learning methods depends strongly on how relevance is defined. In more complex scenarios, such as bimodal distributions, traditional relevance functions struggle to capture rarity, as they assign fixed relevance values based solely on target values, thereby compromising the distinction between truly rare and normal instances. To address these limitations, this study proposes an Instance Hardness-based relevance function (InHaR) for identifying rare instances in regression problems. Unlike traditional relevance functions, the proposed approach incorporates learning difficulty, allowing rarity to be inferred not only from the target distribution but also from the difficulty of instances for the learning algorithm. This property is particularly important in bimodal scenarios, where rarity cannot be accurately inferred from target values alone. Experimental results demonstrate that the InHaR correctly identifies rare regions under bimodal distributions and, when used to guide resampling strategies such as Random Oversampling (RO) and Gaussian Noise (GN), leads to significant improvements in predictive performance compared to traditional relevance-based approaches. The code, dataset, and further details about the proposed method are publicly available at https://github.com/VitorLeitao/instance-hardness-Imbalanced-regression.

</details>


### [230] [On Optimization Complexity of Second-Order Certified Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20192)
*Nikita Doikov,Anastasia Koloskova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从优化角度研究机器遗忘的算法复杂度，提出基于均匀凸正则化器的遗忘误差新界，并开发了一种具有各向异性高斯机制的二阶遗忘算法，在线性模型上实现了更快的认证遗忘收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从优化理论角度正式分析认证遗忘的算法复杂度，揭示遗忘问题与优化难度之间的内在联系。

Method: 利用均匀凸正则化器替代泛化误差推导遗忘前后模型距离的新界；提出带有各向异性高斯机制的二阶遗忘算法，采用拟自协方差损失分析。

Result: 理论上证明了若被移除数据能被遗忘模型良好预测，则对应的优化问题简单；提出的二阶方法在线性模型上达到认证遗忘的快速率，优于一阶方法。

Conclusion: 在逻辑回归和指数回归等应用中，二阶信息能带来认证遗忘性能的可证明收益。

Abstract: We study machine unlearning: the removal of memorized training data from a trained model. Specifically, we investigate the algorithmic complexity of certified unlearning from an optimization perspective. We formalize the goal of an unlearning algorithm as simultaneously achieving certified unlearning and optimization accuracy. Utilizing the notion of uniformly convex regularizers, we prove new bounds on the distance between initial and unlearned models using a novel substitute for generalization error. Thus we theoretically demonstrate that if the removed data is well-predicted by the unlearned model, the corresponding optimization problem is simple. Furthermore, we develop a new second-order unlearning algorithm with an anisotropic Gaussian mechanism and state-of-the-art global convergence. We prove fast rates for our method in achieving certified unlearning for linear models with quasi-self-concordant losses. As a direct application, our theory covers unlearning for logistic and exponential regressions and shows a provable benefit of utilizing second-order information compared to first-order unlearning methods.

</details>


### [231] [OLEDLM: A Unified Language Model for OLED Molecular Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20194)
*Fukang Wen,Yuchong Tang,Jingyuan Li,Beichen Wang,Yixuan Jiang,Xiaoyi Jiang,Yaxuan Liu,Shunyu Wang,Zuoqiang Shi,Yi Zhu,Yanan Zhu,Pipi Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于因果语言模型的逆分子设计框架，专门用于OLED材料生成。该框架采用LLaMA风格的Transformer架构建立基础化学语言模型，结合BERT预训练属性预测器，并通过强化学习优化SMILES序列生成。DFT验证表明，该框架能高效探索OLED化学空间，生成结构有效且光电子性质优化的新型候选分子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OLED材料的开发面临化学空间巨大、量子化学约束严格以及标注数据稀缺等多重挑战。尽管OLED生成问题非常重要，但很少有模型能在此特定领域进行有效训练。现有方法在处理OLED领域特有的严格结构要求方面存在不足。

Method: 采用多阶段策略：(1) 基于LLaMA风格的Transformer架构建立OLED领域的化学基础语言模型；(2) 在大规模OLED数据集上预训练BERT模型，微调属性预测器；(3) 利用属性预测器作为奖励信号，对微调模型进行强化学习以优化SMILES生成；(4) 通过DFT验证评估生成分子的有效性。

Result: DFT验证结果表明，该框架能够高效导航OLED化学空间，生成具有高结构有效性和优化光电子性质的新型候选分子。

Conclusion: 这是首次将LLM成功应用于OLED领域，弥合了通用分子生成与光电子材料严格结构要求之间的差距，为OLED材料的逆设计提供了一种有效的新范式。

Abstract: The development of organic light-emitting diode (OLED) materials faces the compounded challenges of an astronomically large chemical space, stringent quantum-chemical constraints, and a scarcity of labeled data. Although the question of OLED generation is important, few models have been trained effectively for this specific domain. We propose an inverse molecular design framework based on causal language models: given target optoelectronic properties (e.g., excitation energy, oscillator strength), our model directly generates OLED SMILES sequences satisfying the specified constraints. We employ a multi-stage strategy: first, we establish a foundational chemical language model using a LLaMA-style transformer architecture. To the best of our knowledge, this represents the first successful adaptation of LLMs specifically for the OLED domain, bridging the gap between generic molecular generation and the stringent structural requirements of optoelectronic materials. Second, we fine-tune property predictors based on a BERT model pre-trained on our large-scale OLED dataset. Then, we perform Reinforcement Learning on our fine-tuned model, leveraging our property predictor, for better SMILES generation. Finally, through DFT verification, we demonstrate that our framework can efficiently navigate the OLED chemical space, generating novel candidates with high structural validity and optimized optoelectronic properties.

</details>


### [232] [The Quadrilateral Loss: Additivity as a Measurable Behavior of Dense Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20201)
*Antonio Di Cecco*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出四边形损失，一种将加性模型的可加性约束从架构强制转变为可测量行为的可微惩罚项，通过二阶混合差分量化特征交互，实现可加性与准确性之间的可调平衡，并揭示了多种加性约束方法在不同数据 regime 下的表现反转与隐性失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加性模型通过禁止特征交互来换取可解释性，但现有神经实现依赖架构强制这一约束。作者希望将可加性从"架构设计选择"转变为"可测量的行为"，从而更灵活地控制交互程度，并系统比较不同加性约束路径的优劣。

Method: 提出四边形损失（quadrilateral loss），基于训练点对互换单坐标的二阶混合差分，当且仅当该坐标无交互时为零，对分段线性网络仍保持信息量，且期望上等于介入式Shapley-GAM的每坐标交互质量。该损失将可加性变为可调旋钮，并支持在线观测每特征的"投降曲线"（surrender curves）。对比了结构掩码、行为惩罚（可结晶为结构）、权重衰减、回拟合（backfitting）、共享段模型（shared-section model）以及装袋提升树等方法。

Result: 大多数学习到的交互几乎可以免费移除；适度惩罚在小数据集上同时提升准确性和可加性；预正则化的交互大小几乎不能预测正则化后模型保留的交互，破坏了事后交互排名；行为约束优于权重空间约束，且不同数据 regimes 下方法排名反转；收敛的路径在形状函数本身达成一致；发现了三种共享相同解剖结构的隐性失败模式。

Conclusion: 可加性应被视为一种可测量的行为而非仅架构约束；四边形损失提供了灵活的交互控制工具；不同加性约束方法在不同场景下表现差异显著，且存在被忽视的隐性失败模式，其共同特征是将保证条件迁移到静默失效的设定中。

Abstract: Additive models buy interpretability by forbidding feature interactions, a constraint that neural instantiations enforce architecturally. We introduce the quadrilateral loss, a differentiable penalty that treats additivity as a measurable behavior instead: a second-order mixed difference on pairs of training points swapping one coordinate, which vanishes if and only if the coordinate carries no interaction, remains informative for piecewise-linear networks, and equals in expectation the per-coordinate interaction mass of the interventional Shapley-GAM. The loss turns additivity into a dial - most learned interactions prove removable almost for free, and on small datasets a moderate penalty improves accuracy and additivity simultaneously - and into an online observable: its per-feature surrender curves show, across seeds and datasets, that pre-regularization interaction magnitude barely predicts what a regularized model retains, undermining post-hoc interaction rankings. Against this instrument we compare routes to exact additivity, spanning structural masks, behavioral penalties (optionally crystallized into exact structure), weight decay, backfitting, the shared-section model, and bagged boosted stumps: constraining behavior before structure dominates weight-space constraints, rankings reverse between data regimes, and converging routes agree on the shape functions themselves. Three silent failure modes we document share one anatomy: guarantees imported into settings that quietly void their preconditions.

</details>


### [233] [ELSAA: Efficient Low-Rank and Sparse Attention Approximation for Training Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20214)
*Mahdi Heidari,Mohammad Mahdi Rahimi,Jaekyun Moon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ELSAA通过稀疏分支捕获高相似性交互、低秩分支总结全局扩散交互，并引入分母感知融合项来结合两者，从而在不实例化完整二次注意力分数矩阵的情况下实现高效注意力近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Transformer中N×N注意力分数矩阵的二次复杂度瓶颈，该问题阻碍了模型处理更长输入序列的能力。

Method: ELSAA在Q、K、V经密集投影产生后，直接近似注意力分数算子本身：稀疏分支仅关注高相似性交互，低秩分支压缩全局交互；引入分母感知融合项，根据稀疏分支相对于低秩分支的估计注意力质量来缩放稀疏分支，从而归一化支持集不同的两个分支。

Result: 提出了一种无需实例化完整二次分数矩阵即可构建低秩和稀疏注意力输出的实用框架，旨在实现更长上下文的训练。

Conclusion: ELSAA能够同时保留尖锐的token级交互和广泛的上下文混合，为长序列Transformer的高效注意力计算提供了新途径。

Abstract: The quadratic $N\times N$ attention score matrix remains a central obstacle to extending Transformers to longer input lengths. Existing efficient attention methods usually reduce this bottleneck by either imposing sparsity, so that each query attends to only a small subset of keys, or by using low-rank/kernel sketches, so that global interactions are compressed into a lower-dimensional representation. We propose \emph{ELSAA}, an efficient low-rank and sparse approximation of attention. Importantly, ELSAA does \emph{not} decompose the learned projection or output matrices of the Transformer into sparse and low-rank factors. Instead, after dense projections produce $Q,K,V$, ELSAA approximates the induced attention score operator itself: a sparse branch captures selected high-similarity interactions, while a low-rank branch summarizes diffuse global interactions. Since the two branches can be normalized over supports with very different denominator mass, ELSAA introduces a denominator-aware fusion term that scales the sparse branch according to its estimated attention mass relative to the low-rank branch. This gives a practical framework for constructing low-rank and sparse attention outputs without materializing the full quadratic score matrix, aiming to enable longer-context training while preserving both sharp token-level interactions and broad contextual mixing.

</details>


### [234] [User-Centric Modeling of Transactional Sequences with Explainable State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20228)
*Ivan Palagin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出将对比表示学习(CoLES)与状态空间模型(Mamba)混合的方法用于用户中心的事务事件序列建模，通过两种集成策略（初始化隐藏状态或前缀标记）注入用户先验知识，在三个公开数据集上表现优于单独模型，训练速度快2-3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对比学习方法能生成高质量的用户压缩表示，但现有编码器（RNN存在梯度消失问题，Transformer存在二次复杂度问题）在处理长序列时各有局限。Mamba作为选择性状态空间模型能高效处理长程依赖，但在个性化用户分析领域尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出两种混合集成策略：(1) 用CoLES嵌入初始化Mamba的隐藏状态；(2) 将投影后的CoLES嵌入作为前缀标记拼接到输入序列前。两种方法都从第一步开始为模型提供信息丰富的用户先验。采用对比表示学习(CoLES)与选择性状态空间模型(Mamba)的混合架构。

Result: 在Age（多分类年龄组预测）、MBD（多标签产品获取）、Taobao（二分类购买预测）三个公开数据集上，混合模型一致优于独立Mamba和CoLES+线性分类器，训练收敛速度比纯SSM基线快2-3倍。通过离散化步骤图和积分梯度进行可解释性分析，揭示了行为丰富数据集上的选择性事件过滤机制。

Conclusion: CoLES与Mamba的混合模型能够有效结合对比学习的表示能力和状态空间模型的长序列处理优势，在用户建模任务上取得更好的性能和更快的收敛速度，且具备一定的可解释性。

Abstract: We propose a hybrid approach for user-centric modeling of transactional event sequences that combines contrastive representation learning (CoLES) with State Space Models (SSMs). While contrastive methods yield high-quality compressed user representations, existing encoders -- RNNs and Transformers -- suffer from vanishing gradients or quadratic complexity, respectively. Mamba, a selective SSM, efficiently handles long-range dependencies but remains underexplored for personalized user analysis. We investigate two integration strategies: (1)~initializing the Mamba hidden state with a CoLES embedding, and (2)~prepending the projected CoLES embedding as a prefix token to the input sequence. Both approaches supply the model with an informative user prior from the first step. Experiments on three public datasets -- Age (multiclass age-group prediction), MBD (multi-label product acquisition), and Taobao (binary purchase prediction) -- demonstrate consistent improvements over standalone Mamba and CoLES with a linear classifier, with the hybrid models converging 2--3$\times$ faster than the plain SSM baseline. Explainability analysis via discretization-step maps and Integrated Gradients reveals selective event filtering on behavior-rich datasets and identifies the most informative transaction features.

</details>


### [235] [PIER: Physics-Informed Environmental Retrieval for Time-Series Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20230)
*Shiyuan Luo,Runlong Yu,Chonghao Qiu,Yue Qin,Rahul Ghosh,Robert Ladwig,Paul C. Hanson,Yiqun Xie,Xiaowei Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出物理信息环境检索(PIER)框架，通过物理感知流和权重调整机制增强嵌入检索，在356个湖泊的水温和溶解氧预测任务中持续优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境系统建模因观测有限且物理动态跨系统变化而具有挑战性；检索增强方法可跨系统迁移知识，但标准嵌入检索不保证物理过程一致性，相似嵌入可能对应不同物理机制。

Method: PIER是一个模型无关框架，包含：(1)物理感知流，使用基于物理衍生通量特征训练的局部验证器，通过通量-响应一致性评分；(2)权重调整机制，学习每场景权重以自适应平衡嵌入检索和物理感知两个流。

Result: 在美国中西部356个湖泊41年数据上，PIER在水温和溶解氧预测中持续优于基线，且可作为多种骨干模型的通用增强策略。

Conclusion: 物理信息检索能有效保证物理过程一致性，提升环境系统建模的准确性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Accurate modeling of environmental systems is fundamental to scientific understanding and decision-making, yet remains challenging because observations are limited and physical dynamics vary across systems. Retrieval-augmented approaches offer a natural path to transfer knowledge across systems, but standard embedding-based retrieval does not guarantee consistency of underlying physical processes, since scenarios with similar embeddings may arise from different underlying mechanisms. We propose Physics-Informed Environmental Retrieval (PIER), a model-agnostic framework that augments embedding-based retrieval with a physics-aware stream that scores candidates by flux-response consistency with the target, using local verifiers trained on physics-derived flux features. A weight adjustment mechanism then learns per-scenario weights that adaptively balance the two retrieval streams based on diagnostic features summarizing physics-stream reliability.
